




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

anudīpanīpāṭha

1. cittasaṅgahaanudīpanā

anantañ‍ñāṇaṃ  natvāna, laukālaukakaraṃ jinaṃ|

karissāmi paramattha-dīpaniyā nudīpaniṃ||

[tattha, laukālaukakaranti dasasahassilaukadhātuyaṃ catussac‍cadhammadaisanālaukakārakaṃ| paramatthadīpanīti aittha atthau duvidhau padhānatthauca pariyāyatthauca| tattha padhānatthau paramatthaunāma, padhānatthautica padavākyānaṃ mukhyatthau ujukatthau| pariyāyatthaupi kauci, yuttarūpau atthau paramatthau yaiva| paramatthaṃdīpaitipakāsaitītiparamatthadīpanī]| taṃpara matthadīpaniṃkarauntauācariyau pathamaṃtāva buddhassabhagavatau paṇāmaṃkarauti ‘‘udayāyassā’’tiādinā|

tattha ‘‘udayā’’ti udayatauuggamanatau| ‘‘yassā’’ti yassa sabbañ‍ñubuddhamahāsūrassa| ‘‘aikassā’’ti adutīyassa, asadi sassavā| ‘‘saddhammaraṃsijālinau’’ti aitthasaddhammautisatthusā sanadhammauvuc‍cati| tatthaca caturāsītisahassadhammakkhandhasaṅkhātau daisanāsaddhammauidhādhippaitau| saddhammasaṅkhātaṃraṃsijālaṃassa atthīti saddhammaraṃsijālī| tassasaddhammaraṃsijālinau| ‘‘pabujjhiṃsū’’ti vikasiṃsu, catussac‍cañ‍ñāṇavikāsaṃtañ‍ñāṇasamphul‍laṃ pāpuṇiṃsu| ‘‘janambujā’’ti janasaṅkhātāambujā| tatthajanānāma idhabaudhanaiyyasattā adhippaitā, yaisabbañ‍ñudaisanaṃ sutvā catussac‍ca dhammaṃ bujjhissanti| ambujātipadumā| ‘‘jātikkhaittaimahāsaraiti’’ jātikkhaitta saṅkhātai janambujamahāsarai| tattha jātikkhaittaṃ nāmadasasahassa cak‍kavāḷaṃ, yaṃ aikaṃ buddhakkhaittanti vuc‍cati| yattha ca mahābaudhisattānaṃ buddhabhāvatthāya pathamamahābhinīhārakālādīsu aikappahārainapathavikampanādīnipavattanti| yatthacavasantādaivabrahmānau buddhaparisāhaunti| jātikkhaittaimahāsaraiyassa aikassa saddhammaraṃsijālinau mahāsūrassa udayā tasmiṃ jātikkhaittai mahāsarai janambujāpabujjhiṃsūtiyaujanā|

‘‘taṃ mahāsūra’’nti sabbañ‍ñubuddhamahāsūriyamaṇḍalaṃ| janambuja santānaisupavattaṃ mahantaṃ mauhatamaṃnūdati apanaiti, saddhammaraṃsijālaṃ vissaj‍jantau antaradhāpaitīti mahāmauhatamaunudau| taṃ ‘‘mahāmauhatamaunudaṃ’’|

aivaṃ buddhassapaṇāmaṃ katvā attanā icchitaṃ paṇāmappayaujanaṃ pariṇāmaintau ‘‘sañ‍jāta’’ntiādimāha| ‘‘saumahāsūraumayhaṃ hadayai sañ‍jātaṃ tamaukhandhaṃ panūdata’’ntiyaujanā| tattha ‘‘sañ‍jāta’’nti suṭṭhujātaṃ, anamataggaisaṃsārai daḷhaṃ pavattanti atthau| ‘‘tamaukhandha’’nti mahāmauhatamaukhandhaṃ| sabbakilaisatamaukhandhaṃ vā| antarāyakarāni upavīḷakaupaghātakakammānipi tamaukhandhai saṅgahitāni aiva| tathā raugādayau antarāya dhammāpi tamaujātikāaivatamautamaparāyanautiādīsu| ‘‘panūdata’’nti panūdatu, apanaitu, antaradhāpaitu|

aivaṃ sappayaujanaṃ paṇāmaṃ katvā idāni sanidānaṃ ganthappaṭiñ‍ñaṃ karauntau ‘‘paurāṇakaihī’’tiādimāha| tattha nidānaṃ nāma ganthappaṭiñ‍ñāya  āsan‍nakāraṇaṃ| katadhaṃpanatanti, sāratthābhimānīnaṃ yācanañ‍casaṅgahassavipulatthatā ca|

tattha dvīhi gāthāhi sakāraṇaṃ yācanaṃ dassaiti| puna dvīhi gāthāhi saupamaṃvipulatthataṃdassaiti| ‘‘tasmā’’tiādinā ganthappakāra ganthaguṇaihi sahaganthappaṭiñ‍ñaṃ dassaiti| tattha ādigāthāyaṃ ‘‘abhidhammatthasaṅgahai paurāṇakaihi viñ‍ñūhi vaṇṇitā bahūvaṇṇanā idhalaukamhidissantī’’ti yaujanā| ‘‘vaṇṇanā’’ti paurāṇaṭīkāyau vuc‍canti| aivañ‍casati kasmā abhinavaṃ vaṇṇanaṃ yācantīti| ‘‘yai sāratthābhimāninau, tai tāhibahūhi paurāṇavaṇṇanāhituṭṭhiṃ navindanti| tasmā taṃ yācantī’’tiyaujanā| aitaina appasāratthā aiva tāpaurāṇavaṇṇanāyautipi dīpaitiyaiva| tattha ‘‘tuṭṭhi’’nti santuṭṭhiṃ| navindantinapaṭilabhanti| ‘‘yai’’tiyaijanā|

sāratthamaiva abhimānainti, visaisaina nandantisīlaināti sāratthābhimāninau| tainavindantītipurimainasambandhau| puna ‘‘tai’’ti tāhi tuṭṭhiṃ avindantā taijanā| ‘‘ma’’nti attānaṃ niddisati| ‘‘saṅgammā’’ti samāgantvā| yasmā paramatthassadīpanaṃ yācanti, tasmā imissāṭīkāya ‘‘paramatthadīpanī’’ti nāmaṃpi siddhaṃhauti| ‘‘mahaṇṇavai’’ti mahāsamuddai| ‘‘ratanānī’’ti suvaṇṇarajatādīni ratanāni| ‘‘uddharitvā’’ti uddhaṃ āharitvā| ‘‘yathicchakaṃvī’’tiyathicchitaṃpi| yattakaṃ icchanti, tattakaṃvīti adhippāyau| ‘‘daj‍jaiyyuṃ’’ti dadaiyyuṃ| kāmaṃ dadantūti atthau| aiyyādivacanānaṃ anumati atthaipavattanatau| ‘‘navattabbāvaūnatā’’ti mahaṇṇavairatanānaṃ ūnatāhānitā navattabbāva| kasmā, aparimāṇa ratanādhiṭṭhānattā mahāsamuddassa| yatau sau sāgarauti vuc‍cati, sānaṃdhanaratanānaṃ gaihagabbhasadisattāsāgarauti hissa atthau|

‘‘tathaivaitthā’’ti aitasmiṃ abhidhammattha saṅgahaitathaiva| ‘‘vipulatthā’’ti mahantā atthā| ‘‘ratanūpamā’’ti mahaṇṇavai ratanasadisā| ‘‘satakkhattuṃpī’’ti anaikasatavāraṃpi| ‘‘vaṇṇaiyyuṃ’’ti kāmaṃvaṇṇaintu| ‘‘pariyādin‍nā’’ti paritau anavasaisatau ādin‍nā gahitā| parikkhīṇāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘nahaissarai’’ti nahaissanti  nabhavissanti| ‘‘tasmā’’ti yasmāca yācanti, yasmāca pariyādin‍nānahaissanti, tasmā| ‘‘tāsuvaṇṇanāsū’’ti tāsu paurāṇaṭīkāsu| ‘‘vaṇṇana’’nti abhinavavaṇṇanaṃ, abhinavaṭīkaṃkarissanti sambandhau| kīdisaṃvaṇṇanaṃ karissatīti āha ‘‘nānāsārattha sampuṇṇa’’ntiādi| tattha ‘‘uttānapadabyañ‍jana’’nti uttānapadañ‍ca uttānavākyañ‍ca| ‘‘nātisaṅkhaipavitthāra’’nti nātisaṅkhaipaṃnātivitthārañ‍ca| mandā buddhi yaisaṃ tai mandabuddhinau| ‘‘mandā’’ti mudukā| ‘‘buddhī’’ti ñāṇaṃ| mandabuddhinau sautujanai pabaudhaiti vikāsaiti, ñāṇa vikāsaṃpāpaitīti mandabuddhippabaudhanā| ‘‘karissa’’nti karissāmi| ‘‘ta’’nti taṃvaṇṇanaṃ| ‘‘paramatthaisupāṭavatthinausuṇantū’’ti yaujanā| paṭunaubhāvaupāṭavaṃ| paṭunauti byattassapaṇḍitassa| pāṭavaina atthau yaisaṃ tai pāṭavatthinau| itisaddau parisamāpanajautakau| sau hi ganthārabbhavidhānassa idhaparisamāpanaṃ pariniṭṭhānaṃ ñāpaituṃ ganthārabbhavākyassapariyantai yaujitau| avayava vākyānaṃ piyaujīyatiyaiva| aisanayausabbattha|

ganthārabbhagāthāvaṇṇanāniṭṭhitā|

1. aivaṃ ganthārabbhavidhānaṃ katvā idāni ādigāthāya sambandhaṃ dassaintau ‘‘abhidhammatthasaṅgaha’’ntiādimāha| sambandhanti kāraṇapphalasaṃyaugaṃ| tattha gāthāpavattanaṃ kāraṇaṃ nāma| pañ‍capiṇḍattha dassanaṃ phalaṃ nāma| kāraṇapphalasaṃyaugau sambandhaunāma| ‘‘sappayaujanai’’ti phalappayaujanasahitai| ganthaina abhidhātabbau kathaitabbauti ganthābhidhaiyyau| nipatassa kammaṃ nipac‍caṃ| nipac‍ca kiriyā, nipac‍cākārau, nipac‍cakārassakaraṇanti samāsau| ‘‘sā’’ti ratanattayavandanā| dassitātisambandhau| ‘‘abhihitā’’ti kathitā| pakāsitāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘padhānatthabhūtā’’ti adhippaitatthabhūtāti adhippāyau| duvidhauhi atthauvacanatthau ca abhidhānatthauca| tattha gacchatīti gatau, purisauti vuttaigacchati padaina vuttau yaukauci gacchantau vacanatthaunāma| purisauti padainadassitau  padhānatthau adhippaitatthau abhidhaiyyatthau nāmāti| ‘‘abhidhammatthā’’ti|

tattha vuttābhidhammatthā, catudhā paramatthatau|

cittaṃ caitasikaṃ rūpaṃ, nibbānamiti sabbathā|| ti

aivaṃ vuttā abhidhammatthā| aivaṃ vuttattāyaivaca taicattārau abhidhammatthā aiva idhapadhānatthabhūtāti ca, - padhānatthāaiva idhaganthābhidhaiyya bhāvaina adhippaitāti caviñ‍ñāyatīti adhippāyau|

kaicipanavadaiyyuṃ, taiabhidhammatthā saṅgahappakaraṇaṃ pattā visuṃ saṅgahatthānāmabhavaiyyuṃ, naabhidhammatthā nāma| idha ca saṅgahatthā aiva abhidhaiyyabhāvaina adhippaitāti vuttaṃ abhidhaiyyau abhidhammattha saṅgahappadaināti| vuc‍catai| taiabhidhammatthā saṅgahappakaraṇaṃ pattāpi abhidhammatthā aivanāma haunti, na visuṃ saṅgahatthānāma| tattha vuttābhidhammatthātihi vuttaṃ, natuvuttaṃ tattha vuttāsaṅgahatthāti| aivañ‍casati saṅgahitabhāvamattaṃ visiṭṭhaṃ hauti| tadaiva idha abhidhaiyyau nāma siyāti vuttaṃ ‘‘saṅgahitabhāvaupi abhidhaiyyau yaivā’’tiādi| tattha ‘‘saṅgahitabhāvau’’ticittasaṅgahau, caitasikasaṅgahautiādinā saṅgahaṇakiriyā| sā saṅgahitaihi dhammaihi añ‍ñā nahauti| taisvaivadhammaisusaṅgayhatīti vuttaṃ ‘‘saṅgahitabhāvaupi abhidhaiyyau yaivā’’ti| kiñ‍cāpitaihi añ‍ñānahauti, tai svaiva saṅgayhati| sāpana ganthassapadhānatthau nahauti| idha ca padhānatthauva adhippaitauti vuttaṃ ‘‘taṃnasundara’’nti| kasmā nasundaranti āha ‘‘nahisau’’tiādiṃ| aitthacahisaddau imassa vākyassahaituvākyabhāvaṃ jautaiti| aisanayauparatthapi| ‘‘itaupaṭṭhāya cā’’tiādi gantha garudausavivaj‍janaṃ| tattha ‘‘imassasaṅgahassā’’ti imassaabhidhammatthasaṅgahassa| ‘‘dutīyā’’tidutīyāṭīkā| ‘‘dvaipī’’ti dvaipiṭīkāyau| ‘‘visuddhimaggaimahāṭīkā’’ti ācariyadhammapālatthairaina katāparamatthamañ‍jūsānāmaṭīkā| sā brammaraṭṭhai tiriyapabbatavāsinā thairainakataṃ cūḷaṭīkaṃ upādāya mahāṭīkāti pākaṭā| taṃsandhāyaitaṃ vuttaṃ|

ganthappakārauca  pakāravantaihi dhammaihi sahaivasijjhati, vinā nasijjhatīti adhippāyaina ‘‘sauabhidhammatthapadainā’’ti vuttaṃ| kāmañ‍ca sau taihi sahaivasijjhati, vinānasijjhati| abhidhammatthapadaṃ pana saṅgahaṇakiriyāpakāraṃna vadatīti vuttaṃ ‘‘taṃ nasundara’’nti| duvidhaṃ nāmaṃ anvatthanāmaṃ ruḷināmanti| tattha, atthānugataṃ nāmaṃ anvatthanāmaṃ, yathā sukhitassajanassa sukhautināmaṃ| attharahitaṃ āraupitaṃ nāmaṃ ruḷināmaṃ, yathā dukkhitassajanassa sukhauti nāmaṃ| idha pana anvatthanā mantidassaituṃ ‘‘atthānugatā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ‘‘atthānugatā’’ti sakatthānugatā| saddappavattinimittānugatāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘ganthasamañ‍ñā’’ti ganthasammuti| ganthassanāmanti vuttaṃ hauti| saṅgahaganthaunāma pāḷiyaṃtattha tattha vippakiṇṇaidhammai aikattha sabhāgarāsikaraṇavasaina pavattau ganthau| taṃ uggaṇhantau appakaina ganthainabahukaidhammaisukhainajānāti| ‘‘taduggahaparipucchādivasainā’’ti tassauggahauca paripucchācāti dvandau| ādisaddaina dhāraṇādīni saṅgaṇhāti| tattha pāṭhassavācuggatakaraṇaṃ uggahaunāma| uggahi tassapāṭhassa atthaggahaṇaṃ paripucchānāma| ‘‘anāyāsatau’’ti niddukkhaina| ‘‘laddhabbaṃphalānuphala’’nti sambandhau| sarūpatau avabujjhanaṃ sarūpāvabaudhau| ādisaddaina lakkhaṇāvabaudhau rasāva baudhautiādiṃ saṅgaṇhāti| anupādāparinibbānaṃ antau pariyausānaṃ yassāti anupādāparinibbānantaṃ| tattha ‘‘anupādāparinibbāna’’nti taṇhādiṭṭhīhi khandhaisu anupādāyaparinibbānaṃ| anupādisaisa parinibbānanti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘phalānuphala’’nti phalañ‍caiva anuphalañ‍ca| tattha ‘‘phala’’nti mūlapphalaṃ| ‘‘anuphala’’nti paramparapphalaṃ| payaujaitīti ‘‘payaujanaṃ’’| payaujaitīti niyaujaiti| kiṃ niyaujaiti| phalatthikaṃjanaṃ| kattha niyaujaiti| phalanibbattakaikammai| kimatthāya niyaujaiti| tassakammassa karaṇatthāyāti| phalānubhavanatthāya tatthatattha phalānubhavanakic‍caisu payuj‍jīyatīti payaujananti pivadanti| ‘‘sāmatthiyatau’’ti vacanasāmatthiyatau| kiṃ vacanasāmatthiyanti| kāraṇavacanaṃ phalaṃpidīpaiti| phalavacanaṃ kāraṇaṃpidīpaiti| yathātaṃ asukasmiṃ raṭṭhai sammādaivau vuṭṭhauti vuttai taṃ raṭṭhaṃsu  bhikkhanti viñ‍ñāyati| asukaraṭṭhaṃ subhikkhantivuttai tasmiṃ raṭṭhai sammādaivau vuṭṭhauti viñ‍ñāyatīti| payaujanaṃ pana abhidhammattha saddaina dassaitabbaṃ natthi, saṅgahavacanasāmatthiyainaiva siddhaṃ hautīti adhippāyaina ‘‘saṅgahasaddainā’’ti vuttaṃ| sāmatthiyadassanai pana suṭṭhu paripuṇṇavacanaṃ icchitabbaṃ hauti| itarathā aniṭṭhatthappasaṅgaupi siyāti imamatthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘taṃ na sundara’’nti vatvā ‘‘nahī’’tiādinā haituvākyaina tadatthaṃ sādhaiti|

2. aivaṃ sappayaujanai pañ‍capiṇḍatthaiti aittha pañ‍capiṇḍatthai dassaitvā idāni taisaṃpañ‍can‍naṃ piṇḍatthānaṃ visuṃvisuṃ pañ‍cappayaujanāni dassaintau ‘‘tatthā’’tiādimāha| ‘‘tatthā’’ti taisupañ‍casu piṇḍatthaisu| nasaṅkhyātabbanti asaṅkhyaiyyaṃ| saṅkhātuṃasak‍kuṇaiyyanti atthau| napamaitabbanti appamaiyyaṃ| pamaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyanti atthau| aivaṃ kic‍capac‍cayānaṃ katthaci sak‍kattha dīpanaṃ hauti| sakavacanaṃ pāḷivacanaina sādhaituṃ ‘‘yathāhā’’ti pucchitvā pāḷigāthaṃ āhari| tattha ‘‘yathāhā’’ti kathaṃ āha ic‍caivatthau| anantarai vuttassa atthassa sādhakaṃ vacanaṃ kathaṃ pāḷiyaṃ āha, kathaṃ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ āha, kathaṃ ṭīkāyaṃ āhāti aivaṃ yathārahaṃ atthau vaiditabbau| ‘‘taitādisainibbutai akutaubhayai pūjayatau’’ti yaujanā| tattha ‘‘tai’’ti buddha buddha sāvakai| ‘‘tādisai’’ti tathā rūpaisī lakkhandhādiguṇa sampan‍nai| ‘‘nibbutai’’ti kilaisa nibbānaina nibbutai| natthi kutauci haitutau bhayaṃ yaisaṃ tai akutau bhayā| anāgāmi khīṇāsavā| ‘‘bhaya’’nti cittutrāsabhayaṃ| ‘‘pūjayatau’’ti pūjaintassa| ‘‘taṃ payaujana’’ntitassāratanattaya vandanāya payaujanaṃ| ‘‘saṅgahakārā’’ti buddhaghausatthairādayau pacchima aṭṭhakathākārā vuc‍canti| taihi paurāṇaṭṭhakathāsu tattha tattha vippakiṇṇaipakiṇṇakavinicchayaiyuttaṭṭhānaisu saṅgahaitvā abhinava aṭṭhakathāyau karaunti| tasmā sabbāabhinavaaṭṭhakathāyau saṅgahā nāma haunti| tai ca ācariyā saṅgahakārā nāma| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘saṅgahakārāti buddhaghausa| pai॰… vuc‍cantī’’ti| tai antarāya nīvāraṇamaiva icchantīti kathaṃ viñ‍ñāyatīti cai| taisaṃ vacanaina  viñ‍ñāyatīti dassaituṃ saṅgahakāra gāthaṃ āhari| ‘‘ratanattayaikatassa aitassanipac‍cakārassa ānubhāvaina antarāyai asaisatausausaitvāti yaujanā| ‘‘hī’’ti ñāpakahaitu jautakau| ‘‘vutta’’nti aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ vuttaṃ|

3. ‘‘kathañ‍cahautī’’ti sambandhau| iti ayaṃ pucchā| ‘‘vuc‍catai’’ti visaj‍janā kathīyatai| ‘‘hī’’tivitthārajautakau| ‘‘vandanā kiriyābhinipphādakau puñ‍ñappavāhau’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘anaika…pai॰… vārai’’ti ac‍canta saṃyaugatthai upayauga vacanaṃ| ‘‘puñ‍ñābhisandau’’ti puñ‍ñābhisautau, puñ‍ñappavāhauti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘sau ca puñ‍ñātissayau hautī’’ti sambandhau| kasmā sau puñ‍ñā tissayau hautīti| khaitta sampattiyā ca ajjhāsaya sampattiyā ca hautīti dassaituṃ ‘‘anuttaraisū’’tiādimāha| saṃvaḍḍhitthāti saṃvaḍḍhitau| puñ‍ñābhisandau| saṃvaḍḍhitassabhāvau saṃvaḍḍhitattaṃ| sugandhaihiviya suparisuddhaṃvatthaṃ paribhāvīyitthāti paribhāvitau| puñ‍ñābhisandauyaiva| paribhāvitassa bhāvau paribhāvitattaṃ| ubhayatthāpi haitu atthai nissak‍kavacanaṃ| ‘‘mahājutikau’’ti mahātaijau| ‘‘mahapphalau’’ti mūlapphalaina mahapphalau| ‘‘mahānisaṃsau’’ti ānisaṃsapphalaina mahānisaṃsau| ānisaṃsapphalanti ca paramparā phalaṃ vuc‍cati| añ‍ñaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ atik‍kamantau sayati pavattatīti atissayau| puñ‍ñañ‍ca taṃ atissayaucāti puñ‍ñātissayau| atissayapuñ‍ñaṃ, adhika puñ‍ñanti atthau| ‘‘sau anubalaṃ daiti, aukāsalābhaṃ karautī’’ti sambandhau| kathañ‍ca anubalaṃ daiti, kathañ‍ca aukāsalābhaṃ karautīti āha ‘‘sayaṃ payauga sampattibhāvaiṭhatvā’’tiādiṃ| tattha payaugasampattināma atīta puñ‍ñakammānaṃ balavataraṃ upatthambhakakammaṃ hauti| ‘‘bahiddhā’’ti bahiddhasantānatau| ‘‘vipattipac‍cayai sampattipac‍cayai’’ti yaujaitabbaṃ| tattha, vipattipac‍cayānāma-rājatauvā cauratauvā-tiādinā āgatā dukkhuppattipac‍cayā| sampattipac‍cayānāma kāyacittānaṃ sappāya pac‍cayā| cattārau pac‍cayā ca upaṭṭhākakulāni ca ārakkha daivatādayau ca sukhuppatti pac‍cayā| taihi pac‍cayaihi pāmauj‍ja bahu lassa  thairassa santānai rattidivaṃ pītipassaddhisukhasamādhīnaṃ pavattiyā ajjhattabhūtā utucittāhārā ca ati paṇītā haunti| taihi samuṭṭhitā sarīraṭṭhakadhātuyau ca atipaṇītā aiva hutvā upabrūhanti| tattha sarīraṭṭhakadhātuyau nāma pathavi ādayau vātapittasaimhādayauca| ‘‘anubalaṃdaitī’’tiabhinavaṃthāmabalaṃ pavattaiti| ‘‘puñ‍ñantarassā’’ti pavattivipākajanakassa bahuvidhassa puñ‍ña kammassa| ‘‘athā’’ti tasmiṃ kālaiti atthau| ‘‘balavabalavantiyau hutvā’’ti pakati balatau atibalavantiyau hutvā| ‘‘tasmiṃ thairasantānai’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘iṭṭhapphalaghanapūritai’’ti iṭṭhapphalabhūtānaṃ rūpasantatīnaṃ ghanaina pūritai| ‘‘aukāsau nāma natthī’’ti patiṭṭhānaukāsau nāma natthi| ‘‘itī’’ti tasmā| ‘‘dūratau apanītānaiva hauntī’’ti sambandhau| iṭṭhapphalasantānaṃ vibādhanti nīvāraintīti iṭṭhapphalasantānavibādhakāni upapīḷakūpaghātakakammāni| aniṭṭhapphala santāna janakāni, akusala janaka kammāni, apuñ‍ña kammānīti tānitividhāni akusalakammāni dūratau apanītānaiva haunti, taisaṃ vipākassa anaukāsakaraṇaināti adhippāyau| taināha ‘‘nahī’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘tatau’’ti tasmā apuñ‍ña kammānaṃ dūratau apanītattā| abhivādaitabbānaṃ mātāpitu samaṇabrāhmaṇādīnaṃ abhivādanakammaigaruṃkaraṇaṃ abhivādana sīlaṃ nāma| taṃ assa atthīti abhivādanasīlī| guṇavuddhavayavuddhai apacaiti attānaṃ nīcavutti karaṇaina pūjaitisīlaināti vuddhāpacāyī| ubhayatthāpisampadāna vacanaṃ| aivantiādini gamana vacanaṃ| nigamanti ca niṭṭhaṅgamanaṃ| tasmātiādi laddhaguṇa vacanaṃ| laddhaguṇauti ca taṃtaṃpasaṅga visaudhanaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ katvā laddhauvisuddhau atthau vuc‍cati| tathā vacana sāmatthiyaina laddhau atthantaraupi ayamidha adhippaitau| ‘‘nakaivalañ‍ca thairassaiva anantarāyaina parisamāpanatthaṃ hautī’’ti yaujanā| ‘‘sautūnañ‍cagahaṇa kic‍ca sampaj‍janattha’’ntisambandhau| suṇantīti sautārau| taisaṃ| ‘‘gaṇhantāna’’nti uggaṇhantānaṃ| ‘‘vandanāsiddhiyā’’tira tanattayaivandanā puñ‍ñassasiddhitau| javanavinicchayai yasmā antarāya nīvāraṇaṃnāma diṭṭhadhammai icchitabbaṃ phalaṃ hauti| diṭṭha  dhammau ca pathama javanassa vipākakkhaittaṃ| tasmā upatthambhana kic‍caṃ patvāpi pathama javana caitanā aiva idhapariyattāti adhippāyaina ‘‘diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyabhūtā’’ti vuttaṃ| sā pana pathama javana caitanā upatthambhana kic‍caṃ patvāpi sabbadubbalā aivasiyā| kasmā, aladdhāsaivanattā| saisa caitanāyau aiva balavatiyau siyuṃ| kasmā, laddhā saivanattāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘sattajavanapakkhai adhippaitattā upaladdhabbattā taṃ nasundara’’nti sambandhau| ‘‘itī’’ti vākyaparisamāpanaṃ| ‘‘yathā appamattakaṃ hauti| aivamaivaṃ appamattakaṃ hautī’’ti yaujaitabbaṃ| ‘‘tathāhī’’ti tatau aivāti atthau| paṭṭhānaipi=kabaḷīkārauāhārau imassakāyassa āhāra pac‍cayainapac‍cayau=tivibhattau| itarathā ‘kabaḷīkārau āhārau āhārasamuṭṭhānānaṃ rūpānaṃ āhārapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau’ti vibhattau siyāti| ‘‘adhunāvā’’ti imasmiṃ bhavai aiva| aivaṃ vandanāyapayaujanaṃ dīpaitvā idāni saisānaṃ piṇḍatthānaṃ payaujanaṃ dīpaituṃ ‘‘yasmāpanā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha, ‘‘āditauviditaisatī’’tiādimhi-bhāsissaṃ abhidhammattha saṅgaha-nti padaṃ sutvā viñ‍ñātaisati, ‘‘ussāhaujāyati’’| imaṃ uggahaitvā sudul‍labhai abhidhammatthai ca anāyāsaina jānissāma, tammūlakassa ca anupādā parinibbānantassa payaujanassabhāginau bhavissāmāti cittuppāda sambhavatauti adhippāyau|

piṇḍatthānudīpanā niṭṭhitā|

4. padatthai| ‘‘bujjhī’’ti añ‍ñāsi| ‘‘aitthā’’ti aitasmiṃ padai| ca saddau vākyārambha jautakau| vākyā rambhauti ca mūlavākyai yaṃ yaṃ vattabbaṃ avuttaṃ, tassa tassa kathanatthāya anuvākyassa ārambhau| bujjhanakiriyāvuc‍catiñāṇaṃ| kathaṃpanaaviparītatthai pavattau sammāsaddau asaisa byāpanaṃ dīpaitīti pucchāya purimatthamai vabyatiraikatau ca anvayatau ca puna vitthāraintau ‘‘tathāhī’’tiādimāha| tattha, ‘‘tathāhī’’ti tassa vacanassa ayaṃ vitthārauti jautaiti| ‘‘aviparīta’’nti kiriyāvisaisana padamaitaṃ| ‘‘attanau  visayai aivā’’ti attanau ñāṇavisayai aiva taisaṃ visayaucāti sambandhau| yasmā aikaupi dhammau kāladaisasantānādibhaidaina ananta bhaidau hauti| tasmā padaisañāṇikā pac‍caikabuddhādayau aikadhammaṃpi sabbākāratau jānituṃ na sak‍kaunti| taināha ‘‘taihī’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘sabbākāratau’’ti sabhāvatau, haitutau, pac‍cayatau, phalatau, nissandatau, kālatau, daisatautiādinā ākāraina| ‘‘yatthā’’ti yasmiṃ avisayaidhammai| ‘‘tai viparītaṃ bujjhaiyyuṃ, sau avisayau nāmadhammaunatthī’’tiyaujanā| taṃ vitthāraintau ‘‘taihī’’tiādimāha| tattha, ‘‘tiyaddhagatai’’ti tīsukālaisu gatai pavattai| ‘‘addhāmuttakai’’ti kālattayavimuttakai| ‘‘hatthamaṇikaiviyā’’ti hattha talai ṭhapitamaṇiratanāniviya| ‘‘sabbai dhammā’’tiādi pāḷisādhakaṃ| tattha, ‘‘āpāta’’nti abhimukhaṃ patanaṃ| ābādhantipi pāṭhau, auttharitvā upaṭṭhānanti atthau| ‘‘sabbañ‍ñumahābhavaṅga’’nti sabbaisaṃsabbañ‍ñubuddhānaṃ pacchimabhavai paṭisandhitau paṭṭhāya pavattaṃ aṭṭhasu mahāvipākaisu pathamamahāvipākaṃ bhavaṅga cittaṃ| ‘‘tatthā’’ti tasmiṃ mahābhavaṅgai| ‘‘nic‍cakālaṃ upaṭṭhahantī’’ti sabbakālaṃ upaṭṭhānākāra pattā hutvā tiṭṭhanti| kasmā, kassaci āvaraṇassa abhāvatau| idaṃpihi aikaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ nāmāti| ‘‘āvaj‍janāyā’’ti manaudvārāvaj‍jana cittaina| dhammā mahantā| bhavaṅgaṃ parittakaṃ| tasmā parittakaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ aikakkhaṇai mahantānaṃ dhammānaṃ napahautīti caudakassa adhippāyau| ‘‘nacaudaitabbamaita’’nti aitaṃ ṭhānaṃ nacaudaitabbaṃ| ‘‘paramuk‍kaṃsapattāna’’nti aittha ‘‘uk‍kaṃsau’’ti ac‍cuggamau ac‍cuttarau| paramau uk‍kaṃsau paramuk‍kaṃsautiviggahau|

aivaṃ sammāsaddassa atthaṃ vicāraitvā idāni saṃsaddassa atthaṃ vicāraintau ‘‘saṃsaddaupanā’’tiādimāha| tattha, ‘‘upasaggau’’ti upasaggapadaṃ| ‘‘paṭivaidhadhammaisū’’ti paṭic‍casamuppādādīnaṃ paṭivijjhanañ‍ñāṇaisu| natthi ācariyau aitassāti anācariyau| samāsantai kakāraina saha anācariyakau| anācariyakassa bhāvau anācariyakatā| taṃ anācariyakataṃ| tatīyā ruppasamāpatti nāma  ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanajjhānaṃ| taṃ bhagavā āḷārassa santikai uggaṇhāti| catutthāruppasamāpatti nāma naivasañ‍ñā nāsañ‍ñāyatanajjhānaṃ| taṃ udakassasantikai uggaṇhātīti vuttaṃ ‘‘āḷārudakamūlikā’’ti| ‘‘analaṅkaritvā’’ti āvaj‍janasamāpaj‍janādivasaina analaṅkaritvā| anāsaivitvāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘chaḍḍitattā’’ti aitāsamāpattiyaunālaṃ baudhāya, atha khau yāvadaiva tatīya catutthāruppabhavappaṭilābhāya saṃvattantīti aivaṃ ādīnavaṃ disvā chaḍḍitattā| ‘‘bujjhanakiriyāyā’’ti paṭivaidhañ‍ñāṇassa| ‘‘kutau paṭivaidhadhammā’’ti tā kutau paṭivaidhadhammā haunti| paṭivaidhadhammā aiva ca buddhabhāvāyapadaṭṭhānāhauntīti adhippāyau|

pāḷiyaṃ| ‘‘pubbai ananussutaisudhammaisū’’ti imasmiṃ bhavai itau pubbai kassacisantikai ananussutai sucatussac‍ca dhammaisu| ‘‘abhisambujjhī’’tipadai abhisambaudhisaṅkhātaṃ arahattamaggañ‍ñāṇaṃ vuttaṃ| tadaiva ñāṇaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa padaṭṭhānaṃ hauti| tappac‍cayā tadanantarā aivasabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ pātubbhavatīti vuttaṃ ‘‘tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇātī’’ti| ‘‘tadanantarā’’ti ca arahatta maggavīthiyāca catun‍naṃ pac‍cavaikkhana vārānañ‍ca anantarai kālaiti atthau| ‘‘nimittatthai’’ti nimitta haitvatthai| ‘‘bhumma’’nti aṭṭhakathāsuāgataṃ sattamīvibhattiyānāmaṃ| tāsu hi pac‍cattavacanaṃ, upayaugavacanaṃ, karaṇa vacanaṃ, sampadāna vacanaṃ, nissak‍kavacanaṃ, sāmivacanaṃ, summavacananti aivaṃ anuk‍kamaina sattan‍naṃ vibhattīnaṃ nāmāni āgatānīti|

‘‘dasabalañ‍ñāṇaisū’’ti ṭhānāṭhāna kausal‍lañ‍ñāṇādīsu dasa ñāṇabalaisu| ‘‘vasibhāva’’nti aittha attanau vasaṃ vattaituṃ samatthatā saṅkhātausatti visaisau vasaunāma| vasau aitassa atthīti vasī-vasigaṇaihītiādīsuviya| vasinau bhāvau vasibhāvau| taṃ vasibhāvanti atthau| taināha ‘‘vasibhāva’’nti issarabhāvanti| katthaci pana ‘‘vasī’’ti itthiliṅgapadaṃpi dissati=tatrimā pañ‍cavasiyau āvaj‍janavasīsamāpaj‍janavasī=tiādīsu|

sammāsambuddhapada|

5. atulapadai| anaikaihiguṇapadaihi pavattitāvandanā ‘‘anaikaguṇapadavisayānāma’’| ‘‘itikiṃdutīyainā’’ti iti tasmā dutīyaina atulapadaina kiṃ payaujanaṃ atthīti atthau| ‘‘nana samatthā’’ti nasamatthā na hautīti yaujanā| samatthā aivāti adhippāyau| ‘‘mattakārinau’’ti pamāṇakārinau| ‘‘thairau ca taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau’’, tasmā mattaṃ na karauti, dutīyaṃ atula padaṃ āharīti adhippāyau| ‘‘apicā’’ti kiñ‍ci vattabbaṃ atthīti atthau| ‘‘nakaivalaṃ vandanāya antarāyanīvāraṇamaiva icchi tabbaṃ hautī’’ti yaujanā| ‘‘vandanāyā’’ti vandanāhaitu| saupi pañ‍ñāpāṭavādi atthau| ‘‘ganthapārisuddhiyā’’ti ganthadausānāma padadausa vākyadausa atthadausādayau atthi| taihi dausaihi imassa ganthassa pārisuddhiyā| kathaṃ pana vandanāya pañ‍ñāpāṭavādi atthausambhavatīti vuttaṃ ‘‘anussatiṭṭhānaisū’’tiādi| anussatiṭṭhānāni nāma buddha dhamma saṅghasīlādīni| cittasamādhānaṃ āvahatīti cittasamādhānāvahau| ‘‘tikkhāsūrāhutvāvahatī’’ti gambhīraisu attha byañ‍jana padaisu amandā vissaṭṭhā hutvā vahati| ‘‘tadatthāyapī’’ti pañ‍ñāpāṭavādi atthāyapi| guṇanāmapadānaṃ guṇatthaunāmaviggaha vākyaisu pākaṭau, siddhapadaisu apākaṭau| tasmā tāni viggahatthaṃ ajānantānaṃ santikai nāma mattāni sampaj‍jantīti vuttaṃ ‘‘yathāvutta vacanatthayaugaipi…pai॰… pavattattā’’ti| ‘‘sabhāvaniruttiṃ jānantāna’’nti māgadha bhāsaṃ jānantānaṃ| māgadhabhāsāhi mūlabhāsāti ca ariyabhāsāti ca māgadhabhāsāti ca pāḷibhāsāti ca dhammaniruttīti ca sabhāvaniruttīti ca vuc‍cati| ‘‘bhāvatthasuñ‍ña’’nti aittha guṇanāmānaṃ guṇatthau bhāvatthau nāma| sau aivasakatthauti ca vacanatthauti ca viggahatthauti ca vuc‍cati| kiriyanāmādīsūpi aisaivanayau| ‘‘satthū’’ti satthunau| ‘‘samañ‍ñāmatta’’nti nāmasañ‍ñāmattaṃ bhavituṃ nārahati| tathāhi anāthapiṇḍikausaiṭṭhi rājagahaṃ anupattau buddhau laukai uppan‍nauti sutvā udānaṃ udānaisi=ghausaupi khau aisaudul‍labhau laukasmiṃ yadidaṃ buddhau=ti| tasmā buddhauti nāmaṃpi laukai mahantaṃ sudul‍labhaṃguṇapadaṃ hauti| sammāsambuddha nāmaivattabbamaivanatthīti| ‘‘sabhāvaniruttiṃ  ajānantānaṃ pana padasahassaṃ vuc‍camānaṃpī’’ti tiṭṭhatu aikaṃ atulapadaṃ, padasahassaṃpi vuc‍camānaṃ satthusamañ‍ñāmattamaiva sampaj‍jati| tādisāhi janā idaṃ laukai mahantaṃ guṇapadantipi najānanti| bhāvatthaṃ kiṃjānissanti|

‘‘atulau’’ti añ‍ñaina sau asadisauti vā, añ‍ñau vātaina sadisautassanatthītivā, - dvidhāpiatthaulabbhati| sādhakagāthāyaṃ ‘‘paṭipuggalau’’ti yugaggāhīpuggalau| kiñ‍cāpi makkhali pūraṇādayau visuṃvisuṃ - ahaṃ sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī - tica, ahaṃ sammāsambuddhau-tica paṭijānantā yugaggāhinau hutvā vicaranti| dhammatau pana sinairu pabbata rājassa santikai sakkhara kathalāniviyasampaj‍jantīti| ‘‘anacchariya’’nti natāva accharitabbaṃ hautīti atthau| ‘‘buddhabhūtassā’’ti buddhabhāvaṃ bhūtassapattassa| ‘‘yaṃ buddha bhūtassa atulattaṃ, aitaṃ anacchariya’’nti yaujanā| yadi caitaṃ anacchariyaṃ hauti, katamaṃ pana tāva acchariyaṃ bhavatīti āha ‘‘sampatijātassā’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘sampatijātassā’’ti aj‍jaivajātassapi assa bhagavatau| kathaṃ atulatā pañ‍ñāyatīti āha ‘‘tadāhī’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘aikaṅgaṇānī’’ti aikatalāni| tadāhi baudhisattassapuñ‍ñānubhāvaina atimahantau aubhāsau pātubbhavati| = uḷārau aubhāsau pāturahausi atik‍kammadaivānaṃ daivānubhāva=ntihi vuttaṃ| taina aubhāsaina pharitā sabbai pathavi pabbatādayau jātiphalikakkhandhāviya suppasan‍nā haunti| dasasahassa cak‍kavāḷāni aikatalaṃ hutvā pañ‍ñāyanti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘anaikāni cak‍kavāḷasahassāni aikaṅgaṇāni ahaisu’’nti| ‘‘paramāya pūjāyāti thutimaṅgalavacanapūjāya| ‘‘laukassā’’ti sabba sattalaukatau| aivaṃ acchambhi vācaṃ nicchāraisi dhammatāya sañ‍cauditattāti adhippāyau| tattha ‘‘acchambhivāca’’nti visāradavācaṃ| āsabhiṃ vācantipi pāṭhau| uttamavācanti atthau| ‘‘nicchāraisī’’ti udāharati| idampi anacchariyaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ pitatau acchariyataraṃ atthīti dassaituṃ ‘‘yadāpanā’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| pāramitā guṇaihi taina sadisau kaucinatthaiva thapaitvā añ‍ñai ca mahābaudhi sattaiti adhippāyau| ‘‘assā’’ti taina sadisassa| ‘‘natthibhāvau dīpaitabbau’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘dīpaitabbau’’ti buddhavaṃsapāḷitau āharitvā dīpaitabbau| ‘‘kutausāvakabaudhisattānaṃ satasahassaṃ sakkhissatī’’ti yaujanā| pāramiyau pakārainavicinanti aitaināti pāramipavicayau| ñāṇaṃ| taṃpanañāṇaṃ mahābaudhisattānaṃ aiva uppan‍naṃ nahauti| pac‍caikabaudhisatta sāvakabaudhisattānaṃpi uppan‍namaiva| tadaiva ca sabbaisaṃpi baudhisattānaṃ niyatabyākaraṇappaṭilābhai padhānakāraṇanti dassaituṃ ‘‘sāvakabaudhisattāpī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha baudhivuc‍cativimaukkhañ‍ñāṇaṃ| ariyamaggassaitaṃ nāmaṃ| baudhimhisajanti laggantīti baudhisattā| ‘‘laggantī’’ti tappaṭilābhatthāya niyata cittā hauntīti atthau| baudhi atthāya paṭipan‍nā sattā baudhisattātipi yuj‍jati| buddha suñ‍ñaipilaukai kammassakatāñāṇai ṭhatvā vaṭṭadukkhatau maukkhadhammapariyaisinau sattāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘sambhāra dhammai’’ti dasavidhai pārami dhammai| vaṭṭaṃ anusaranti anugacchantīti vaṭṭānusārinau| pathaviyaṃ paṃsucuṇṇāni viya pakatiyā vaputhubhūtājanāti puthuj‍janā| mahantāputhuj‍janāti mahāputhuj‍janā| vaṭṭānusārinau ca tai mahāputhuj‍janācāti samāsau| taisaṃ bhāvauti viggahau| ayaṃ bhāvauyaiva taisaṃ bhūmīti ca vuc‍cati| atthatau pana maukkhadhammanirapaikkhatā aiva| acchandikatātipi vuc‍cati| ‘‘auk‍kantā’’ti paviṭṭhā| tayauniyatā, baudhisattaniyatau ca cūḷasautāpan‍naniyatau ca ariya sautāpan‍naniyatau ca| tattha baudhisattaniyatau baudhisambhārabalaina siddhau| cūḷasautāpan‍na niyatau pac‍cayākārānubaudhañ‍ñāṇabalaina| ariyasautāpan‍na niyatau sautāpatti maggañ‍ñāṇa balaina| taisu baudhisattaniyatau idha adhippaitauti vuttaṃ ‘‘aikaina pariyāyainā’’tiādi| vattabbamaivanatthitaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ baudhisattānaṃ pārami pavicayañ‍ñāṇa sampattiyā vinā niyatabyākaraṇa lābhā saṅkāya aiva abhāvatauti adhippāyau|

padasiddhivicāraiyaṃ vuttaṃ vibhāvaniyaṃ=tulāyasamitautulyau| tulyau aiva tulauyakāra laupavasainā=ti| taṃsandhāya ‘‘ya kārassavāvasainā’’ti  vuttaṃ| yañ‍cavuttaṃ tatthaiva=athavāsamītatthai akārapac‍cayavasaina tulāyasamītautulau=ti| taṃ sandhāya ‘‘akārassavāvasainā’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha ‘‘tulāyā’’ti laukai dhāraṇatulāsadisāya pañ‍ñāyāti atthau| ‘‘samītau’’ti samaṃ katau| nahi tulasaddau bhavituṃ nayuttau| yuttau aivāti adhippāyau| kathaṃ viñ‍ñāyatīti āha ‘‘tulayitu’’ntiādiṃ| tatthahi ‘‘tulayituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyau’’tivacanaina tassakammasādhanattaṃdassaiti| ‘‘kammasādhanainaivā’’ti pubbai-tulayitabbau añ‍ñaina saha pamitabbauti tulauti aivaṃ idhavuttaina kamma sādhana vacanatthainaiva| ‘‘tadatthasiddhitau’’ti tassa vibhāvaniyaṃ vuttassa duvidhassa atthassa siddhitau| ‘‘tatau’’ti tulasaddatau| cintāya kiṃ payaujanaṃ atthi| natthiyaivāti adhippāyau| vadati sīlaināti vattā| vādī puggalau| vattunau icchāvatticchā| vattuṃ icchāvatticchātipivadanti| vatticchaṃ anugatau sammuti saṅkaitavauhāra siddhattāti samāsau| ‘‘aita’’nti aitaṃ dvidhāsiddhavacanaṃ| ‘‘cai’’ti caivadaiyya| ‘‘nā’’ti nayuttaṃ| ‘‘yathāsuta’’nti tula iti sutaṃ| ‘‘yutta’’nti yathāsuta niyāmainaiva yuttaṃ vaj‍jaitvā| ‘‘assutassā’’ti dhāraṇatulāpariyāyassa itthiliṅgatulāsaddassa| tatauyaivayakāra yuttassatulyasaddassa ca assutassa| itthiliṅgaisati, tataupi aikau akārauti katvā samītatthai dutīyau taddhita akāraupi assutauyaivanāmahauti| ‘‘parikappanāyā’’ti parikappaitvā kathanāya| payaujanābhāvatau na yuttantisambandhau| atulapadaṃ|

6. aivaṃ dvin‍naṃ padānaṃ padattha saṃvaṇṇanaṃ katvā idāni taisaṃyaiva atthuddhārasaṃvaṇṇanaṃ karauntau ‘‘imaihi panā’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘sampadā’’ti sampattiyau| ‘‘baudhisambhārasambharaṇaṃ nāma’’ samatiṃ sapāramīnaṃ paripūraṇaṃ| ‘‘mahāvajirañ‍ñāṇa’’nti bhagavatau āsavakkhayañ‍ñāṇampi vuc‍cati| tassa pubbabhāgai buddhabhāvatthāya anupadadhammavipassanāvasaina chattiṃsa kauṭi satasahassa saṅkhānaṃ daivasikaṃ vaḷañ‍janakapphalasamāpattīnaṃ pubbabhāga vipassanāñāṇampi mahāvajirañ‍ñāṇanti  vuc‍cati| sabbampaitaṃ mahāṭīkāyaṃ vuttaṃ| ‘‘mahābaudhiyā’’ti sabbañ‍ñu buddhānaṃ abhisambaudhi saṅkhātassa aggamaggañ‍ñāṇassa| pahiyyanti pahātabbā dhammā aitaināti pahānaṃ| pajahanti pahātabbaidhammaiaitainātivā pahānanti katvā taṃ aggamaggañ‍ñāṇampitaṃ vipassanāñāṇampi pahānanti vuc‍catīti iminā adhippāyaina ‘‘pahānasampadāyaṃ vā sā saṅgahitā’’ti vuttaṃ| pañ‍casīlāni| pāṇātipātassa pahānaṃsīlaṃ, vairamaṇisīlaṃ, caitasikaṃsīlaṃ, saṃvarausīlaṃ, avītik‍kamausilantiādīsuviya aitthahi pahānasīlaṃ nāma yathā vuttaina atthaina vairamaṇisīlamaivāti yuj‍jati| pahānaṃ nāma kaucidhammau nahautīti adhippāyai pana satipahāna sīsaina pahānasādhakaṃ tadaivañāṇadvayaṃ upacārainapahānanti gahaitabbaṃ| itarathā pahānasampadā nāma asārā aphalāti āpaj‍jaiyyāti| pac‍caikabuddha buddhasāvakā kilaisai pajahantāpi vāsanāya saha appajahanatau citta santānai mauhavāsanāya vij‍jamānattā sabbañ‍ñu bhāvaṃ nagacchanti| tasmā yathātaisaṃ kilaisappahānaṃ pahānasampadā nāma nahauti| natathāsabbañ‍ñubuddhānanti āha ‘‘sahavāsanāyā’’tiādiṃ| vibhāvaniyaṃñāṇasampadā pathamaṃ vuttā| tatau pahāna sampadā| ṭīkāyaṃ pana pahānasampadā pathamaṃ vuttā| tatau adhigama sampadānāma vuttā| tatau ñāṇasampadā| pahānasampadāyañ‍ca aggamaggañ‍ñāṇaṃ dassitaṃ| adhigama sampadāti ca sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇappaṭilābhau vuttau| ñāṇasampadāyampana taihi dvīhi ñāṇaihi avasaisānidasabalañ‍ñāṇādīnisabbañ‍ñāṇānidassitāni| idhapi ṭīkānayamaiva sambhāvaintau ‘‘pahānasampadāyaivapanā’’tiādimāha| ‘‘sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇappadaṭṭhāna’’nti sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassapadaṭṭhānaṃ, āsan‍na kāraṇaṃ| ‘‘na hi maggañ‍ñāṇatau añ‍ñā pahānasampadānāma atthi’’| paramatthatau natthīti adhippāyau| idañ‍ca visuddhimaggai=pahānanti kauci dhammaunāma natthi añ‍ñatra vuttappakārānaṃ pāṇātipātādīnaṃ anuppādamattatau=ti āgatattā vuttaṃ| pahāyakadhammasamādānaina pana pahātabba dhammānaṃ anuppādau nāma aikaupaṇīta dhammauhauti| aikaṃ santi padaṃ hauti| tathāhi vuttaṃ paṭisambhidā maggai=uppādau bhayaṃ, anuppādau khaimanti santipadai ñāṇaṃ| pavatti bhayaṃ, appavatti khaimanti santipadaiñāṇa=nti| tadaṅgappahānaṃ panatadaṅgaanuppādau nāma| vikkhambhanappahānaṃ vikkhambhana anuppādau nāma| samucchaidappahānaṃ samucchaida anuppādau nāmāti vattabbaṃ| idha pana anuppāda sampāpakaṃ vipassanā ñāṇañ‍ca maggañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca upacāraina pahānanti adhippaitaṃ| kasmā, upariñāṇa sampadādīnaṃ pac‍cayattāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘sampadāsaṅkarau’’ti sampadāsambhaidau, sampadāsammissau| ‘‘ñāyāgata’’nti yuttitau āgataṃ| ‘‘sīlādiguṇaihī’’ti sīla samādhi pañ‍ñā vimutti vimuttiñ‍ñāṇadassana guṇaihi| ‘‘iddhidhammaihī’’ti iddhividhābhiñ‍ñādīhi iddhiguṇaihi| ‘‘lakkhaṇānubyañ‍janappaṭimaṇḍitassā’’ti dvattiṃsa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi ca asīti khuddakalakkhaṇaihi ca paṭimaṇḍitassa| ‘‘āsayau’’ti citta santānai adhisayitau icchāvisaisau| ‘‘ajjhāsayassā’’ti alaubhajjhāsayādikassaajjhāsayassa| ‘‘uḷāratā’’ti paṇītatā| ‘‘hitajjhāsayatā’’tihitakāmatā| aparipāka gatindriyānaṃ sattānaṃ indriyaparipākakālāgamanañ‍ca aittha vattabbaṃ| ‘‘abhiñ‍ñātāna’’nti atipākaṭānaṃ| ‘‘dvaipahānasampadā’’ti dvaipahāna sampadā ñāṇasampadā| sammāsambuddhapadai| ‘‘sāmaṃ sac‍cāni abhisambujjhī’’ti aittha abhisambaudhisaṅkhātaṃ aggamaggañ‍ñāṇaṃ gahitaṃ| tañ‍ca pahānakic‍cappadhānaṃ hauti| ‘‘tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattau’’ti aittha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘balaisu ca vasibhāva’’nti aittha dasabalañ‍ñāṇāni gahitāni| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘dvai…pai॰… sammāsambuddhapadainavibhāvitā’’ti|

sampadāniṭṭhitā|

7. sasaddhammagaṇuttamapadai| yathā=sasaṅghaṃlaukanāyakaṃ namassissaṃ=ti aitthasahasaddassa samavāyatthattā ahaṃlaukanāyakañ‍ca saṅghañ‍ca namassissanti aivaṃ samavāyatthau viñ‍ñāyati| tathā idhapi sammāsambuddhañ‍casaddhammañ‍ca gaṇuttamañ‍ca abhivādiyāmīti aivaṃ kiriyāsamavāyatthau sahasaddaina dīpitauti dassaituṃ ‘‘dūratauhaṃ…pai॰… aivamidaṃ daṭṭhabba’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘ida’’nti sasaddhammagaṇuttamapadaṃ| aittha  ca‘‘samavāyau’’ti dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ bahūnaṃ vā atthānaṃ aikasmiṃ dabbaivā guṇaivākiriyāyavāsamaṃ avaic‍ca ayanaṃpavattanaṃ samavāyau| pac‍cānutāpa pac‍cānumaudanādiṭhānaisu=ahaṃ pubbaidānaṃ nadadissaṃ, sīlaṃ narakkhissaṃ| - anaikajāti saṃsāraṃ sandhāvissa=ntiādinā atītaipikālai anāgatavacanaṃ payuj‍jatīti āha ‘‘namassissa’’nti namassiṃti| ‘‘guṇībhūtāna’’nti samāsapadai visaisanabhūtānaṃ, appadhānabhūtānanti atthau| abbhūta tabbhāvaicāyaṃ īkārau| yathā, kākau saitī bhavati, bakau kaṇhībhavatīti| aittha ca ‘‘saitī bhavatī’’ti asaitapubbau saitaubhavati| ‘‘kaṇhībhavatī’’ti akaṇhapubbau kaṇhaubhavatīti atthau| tathā idhapi| buddhaṃ dhammañ‍ca saṅghañ‍cavanditvā-tiādīsu visuṃvisuṃ padhānattā aguṇabhūtāpi dhammasaṅghā idhasamāsapadai añ‍ñapadatthassaguṇabhūtāhaunti| ayaṃ abbhūtatabbhāvatthau nāma| ‘‘abhivāditabhāvau’’ti vuttai tapac‍cayassa bahulaṃ atītakālavisayattā pubbaiganthārambhakālai dhamma saṅghānaṃpi thairassa vandanāsiddhi dassitā hauti| ‘‘abhivādaitabba bhāvau’’ti vuttaipana tabbapac‍cayassakālasāmañ‍ñavisayattāna tathā dassitā hauti| dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, sīlaṃ rakkhitabbantiādīsu viya dhammasaṅghānāmasabbakālaṃpi abhivādaitabbāti| aivaṃ dhammasaṅghānaṃ sabbakālaṃpi abhivādanārahaguṇau aiva dassitauti imamatthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tatthā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha, ‘‘abhivādana’’nti idaṃ kiriyāsamavāyadassanatau vuttaṃ| kālavisaisaṃ pana nadīpaitiyaiva| ‘‘attanau nidassanainā’’ti saputtadārau āgatauti attanā nīharitvā dassitaina payaugaina| sauhi payaugau kiriyā samavāyassaiva| naguṇasamavāyassāti| aittha ca thairassa vandanāvacanai thairau imaihi yaivapadaihi ratanattayaṃtīhidvārai hivandatīti gahaitvā ‘‘abhivādiyā’’ti aittha abhivādiyādhīti ca atthaṃ nīharanti| appadhāna kiriyāpadai pana tadattha nīharaṇaṃ asambhāvaintau ‘‘apicā’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘apicā’’ti kiñ‍ci vattabbaṃ atthīti atthajautanai ayaṃ nipātasamudayau| ‘‘ganthappaṭiñ‍ñāyā’’ti ganthappaṭiñ‍ñāvacanaina| ‘‘sahaghaṭaitvā’’ti aikatau sambandhitvā|

vacanatthai| sadhanau purisau, dhanavāpurisau-ti ādayau samāsataddhitasaddā yaibhūyyaina atissayattha dīpakā haunti| nahi appakaina dhanaina tathā vauharanti| tasmā idhāpi tathā rūpaṃ atissayatthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘attanānimmitaina…pai॰… saraṇabhūtainā’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha, ‘‘attanānimmitainā’’ti attanā uppāditaina| ‘‘nahī’’tiādinā tadatthamaiva byatiraikatau vivarati| tattha, ‘‘paranimmitainā’’ti buddhanimmitaināti adhippāyau| ‘‘tathā thaumana’’nti sasaddhamma gaṇuttamanti thaumanaṃ| ‘‘idaṃpī’’ti dutīyattha sampiṇḍanai ayaṃpikārau| nakaivalaṃ purimapadadvayamaiva satthu asādhāraṇaguṇapadaṃ hauti| atha khau idaṃpi padaṃ satthu pac‍caikabuddhādīhi asādhāraṇa padamaivahautīti yaujanā|

dhammavacanatthai| ‘‘dhāraitī’’ti vahati| gāthāyaṃ ‘‘rakkhatī’’ti apāyādidukkhataurakkhati| ‘‘yaisa’’nti kilaisānaṃ| ‘‘imasmiṃ atthai’’ti kilaisasamucchindanasaṅkhātai dhāraṇatthai| ‘‘nibbānañ‍canippariyāyatau dhammau nāmā’’ti kasmā vuttaṃ, nanu nissaraṇappahānamaiva nibbānassa kic‍caṃ, idañ‍ca samucchaidappahānanti caudanaṃ pariharantau ‘‘ariyamaggāhī’’tiādimāha| ‘‘nibbānaina sahaiva hutvā’’ti ārammaṇādhipatibhūtaṃ ārammaṇū panissayabhūtañ‍ca nibbānaṃ attanau patiṭṭhaṃ katvāti adhippāyau| apica, samucchaidauti ca nissaraṇanti ca atthatau samānagatikaṃ hauti| tasmā nissaraṇaṃpi mukhyadhāraṇa maivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| nissaraṇamaivavā padhānadhāraṇanti piyuj‍jatiyaiva| ‘‘dhāraṇūpāyauyaivahauti’’| namukhyadhāraṇaṃ| kasmā, samucchaida kic‍cābhāvatau| ‘‘aitaipañ‍ca pariyāyadhammāyaiva’’| kasmā, nibbānassaviyamaggānaṃ samucchaida kic‍cai asahāyattāti| aittha ca ‘‘sāmañ‍ñapphalānī’’ti samaṇassa bhāvau sāmañ‍ñaṃ| ariyamaggassaitaṃ nāmaṃ| sāmañ‍ñassaphalaṃ sāmañ‍ñapphalaṃ| dutīyavikappai akic‍ca pac‍cayabhūtāpi kaicikitakapac‍cayā kammatthaigatā kic‍capac‍cayānaṃpi atthaṃ dīpaiti| yathā, diṭṭhaṃ, sutaṃ, mutaṃ, viñ‍ñātanti vuttaṃ ‘‘dhāraṇārahau’’ti| ‘‘yathā vutta dhammā yaivā’’ti pañ‍camukhyadhammā, pañ‍capariyāyadhammāyaiva| ‘‘kaicī’’ti cattārau maggā| puna ‘‘kaicī’’ti nibbānamaiva| bahuvacana sautaipatitattā  aittha bahuvacanaṃ ruḷaṃ| puna ‘‘kaicī’’ti cattārau sāmañ‍ñapphala dhammā| ‘‘kaicī’’ti pariyatti dhammau| aitthāpi bahuvacanaṃ sautapatitamaiva| ‘‘dhārainta’’nti dhāraintaṃ puggalaṃ| tatīya vikappai ‘‘apatamānaṃ vahantī’’ti apatamānaṃ katvā vahanti| catuttha vikappai ‘‘aitthā’’ti aitasmiṃ dhammai| dhammauvadīpaṃ aitaisanti dhammadīpā| dhammauva paṭisaraṇaṃ aitaisanti dhammappaṭisaraṇā| dhammadīpā bhikkhavai bhavatha dhammappaṭisaraṇā, anañ‍ñappaṭisaraṇā tihi vuttaṃ| laddhā patiṭṭhā aitaisanti laddhappatiṭṭhā| yuj‍jatiyaiva| dhammadīpapāṭhānulaumattāti adhippāyau| dhammavicāraṇāyaṃ, caudakaupaṭipatti dhammaṃ dasavidha dhammatau añ‍ñaṃmañ‍ñamānau ‘‘kasmā’’tiādinā caudaiti| sau pana paṭipatti dhammau tatau añ‍ñauna hauti, tatthaiva antaugadhauti dassaintau ‘‘saupanā’’tiādimāha| ‘‘maggassa pubbabhāgappaṭipadā hautī’’ti yathā ambarukkhau ambapupphaambapphalassa patiṭṭhā bhāvaina pubbabhāga nissayau hauti| kasmā, itauyaivatassa pupphapphalassa jātattā aitthaivasaṃ vaḍḍhitattā ca| tathā paṭipatti dhammaupi ariyamaggapphalassapatiṭṭhābhāvaina pubbabhāgūpanissayappaṭipadāhauti| kasmā, itauyaiva tassajātattā aitthaivasaṃ vaḍḍhitattā ca| vuttañhaitaṃ mahāvagga saṃyuttai| saiyyathāpi bhikkhavai yaikaici maibījagāmabhūtagāmāvuḍḍhiṃ viruḷiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti| sabbaitai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviṃ patiṭṭhāya| aivamaiva khau bhikkhavai bhikkhusīlaṃ nissāya sīlaipatiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgīkaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau vuḍḍhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisūti| ‘‘pubbacaitanāviyadānai’’ti yathā tividhaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñanti vuttai dānavatthu pariyaisanatau paṭṭhāya dānaṃ ārabbhapavattā sabbā pubbabhāga caitanā dānavacanai saṅgahitā dānantvaiva saṅkhyaṃgatā| aivaṃ sau paṭipatti dhammau ariya maggavacanai aivasaṅgahitau, ariyamaggau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gatauti vuttaṃ hauti|

imasmiṃ dhammasaṃsandanai aparampi vattabbaṃ vadantau ‘‘apicā’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘yadaggainā’’ti yainakāraṇakauṭṭhāsaina dhāraṇūpāyautiādinā kāraṇabhāgaināti atthau| vandanaṃ araha tīti  vandanaiyyau| tasmiṃ vandanaiyyai| ‘‘puthuj‍janakalyāṇakau’’ti aittha tividhau kalyāṇakau vinayakalyāṇakau suttantakalyāṇakau abhidhammakalyāṇakauti| tattha vinayai pañ‍ñattāya kalyāṇappaṭipattiyā saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayakalyāṇakau nāma| sau idha bhikkhu pātimaukkha saṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāra gaucara sampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisūti aittha vaiditabbau| tattha vinayai pañ‍ñattākalyāṇappaṭipattiduvidhā, ādi brahmacariyakasīlaṃ abhisamacārikasīlanti| tattha, ubhatauvibhaṅgapariyāpan‍naṃ sīlaṃ ādibrahmacariyakaṃ nāma| khandhakapariyāpan‍naṃ sīlaṃ abhisamacārikaṃ nāma|

suttantaisu vuttāya kalyāṇappaṭipattiyā saman‍nāgatau gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā suttanta kalyāṇakau nāma| sau catūhi bhikkhavai dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sabbaṃ duggatibhayaṃ samatik‍kantau hauti| katamaihi catūhi| idha bhikkhavai ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti iti pisau bhagavā…pai॰… satthādaivamanussānaṃ, buddhau, bhagavāti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hautīti aittha vaiditabbau| tattha buddhai avaic‍cappasādau nāma arahaṃ, sammāsambuddhau, tiādikānaṃ guṇapadānaṃ attha jānanañ‍ñāṇaina yuttau pasādau| ariyaihikāmīyanti icchīyantīti ariyakantāni| suparisuddhassa ājīvaṭṭhamakasīlassaitaṃ nāmaṃ| cattārimāni bhikkhavai sautāpattiyaṅgāni| katamāni cattāri| sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammassavanaṃ, yaunisau manasikārau, dhammānu dhammappaṭipattītiādīni bahūni suttantāni idha vattabbāni|

abhidhammai vā suttantaisu vā vuttāya kalyāṇappaṭipattiyā saman‍nāgataugahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā abhidhammakalyāṇakau nāma| sau idha sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariya dhammassakauvidau  ariyadhammaisu vinītau| sau rūpaṃ attatau na samanupassatīti aittha vaiditabbau| tattha, ‘‘sutavā’’ti aittha khandhā, yatana, dhātu, paṭic‍casamuppāda, satipaṭṭhānā, dīsu uggaha paripucchā vinicchayañ‍ñāṇa saman‍nāgatau gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā suta vā nāmāti aṭṭhakathāsu vuttau| sau aiva puthuj‍jana kalyāṇakauti ca vuc‍cati|

duvai puthuj‍janā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

andhau puthuj‍janau aikau, kalyāṇaikau puthuj‍janau|| ti

ca vuttaṃ| aṅguttarai pana catuk‍kanipātai katamau puggalau appassutau sutaina upapan‍nau hauti| idhaikac‍cau dhammaṃ suṇāti aikāyapi cātuppadikāya gāthāya attha mañ‍ñāya dhamma mañ‍ñāya dhammā nu dhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ appassutausutaina upapan‍nauti vuttaṃ| sutavāti ca sutaina upapan‍nauti ca atthatau aikanti| puthuj‍jana kalyāṇakau saṅghai saikkhaisusaṅgahitau| vuttaṃhaitaṃ parivārai kai sikkhantīti| puthuj‍jana kalyāṇakaina saddhiṃ satta ariyapuggalā sikkhanti| arahā khīṇāsavau sikkhitasikkhauti| dakkhiṇa vibhaṅgasutta aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana| ti saraṇa saraṇaṃ gatau upāsakaupi sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nai saṅgahitauti vuttaṃ| tattha, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍naunāma sautāpatti maggaṭṭhau ariyapuggalau| ‘‘sauhisaṅgahitau’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘aittāvatā paṭikkhittaṃ hautī’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘saṅghai asaṅgahitau’’ti saṅghaṃsaraṇaṃ gacchāmīti ca, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghauti ca, aivarūpaisu ṭhānaisu āgatai saṅgha vacanai asaṅgahitau| ‘‘na hitaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchantassasaraṇagamanaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti idaṃ saṅghaṃsaraṇaṃ gacchāmīti aittha saṅgha vacanaitassapuggalassa asaṅgahita bhāvasādhanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| taināha ‘‘taṃ paṭikkhittaṃ hautī’’ti| sacai pana sabbaṃ bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghaṃ anuddhissatamaiva puggalaṃ saṅghaṃsaraṇaṃ gacchaiyya āyasmantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmīti| saraṇa gamanaṃ na sampaj‍jatiyaiva| atha sabbaṃ bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghaṃ uddissa tassa santikai saraṇaṃ gacchaiyya saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmīti| sampaj‍jatiyaiva| idañ‍ca daharakālai sabbappathamaṃ saraṇagamanaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| idañhi saraṇa gamanaṃ nāma sakiṃ gahaitvā ratanattayai saddhaṃ ajahantassa yāvajīvaṃpi na bhij‍jati| punappunaṃ gahaṇa kic‍caṃ natthi| punappunaṃ gaṇhantaipi dausaunatthi| punappunaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ vaḍḍhati| sabbappathamaṃ gahaṇakālai ca añ‍ñaina din‍nattā laddhaṃ nahauti| attanauvacī bhaidaina laddhaṃ hauti| tasmā añ‍ñassa santikai aggahaitvā sayamaiva vacībhaidaṃ gaṇhantassa gahaṭṭhassa saraṇa gamanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva| tathā sabbāni gahaṭṭhasīlānīti| sāmaṇaira saraṇa gamanampana bhikkhunādin‍namaiva labbhati| tañ‍ca khau ubhin‍naṃpiṭhānakaraṇa sampattiyā sati aivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘vau’’ti tumhākaṃ| yaudhammau ca daisitau yauvinayaucapañ‍ñattau| ‘‘mamac‍cayainā’’ti mamātik‍kamaina| mayi parinibbutaiti vuttaṃ hauti| satthā bhavissatīti pāṭhasaisau| ‘‘saṃvaṇṇitau’’ti suṭṭhutaraṃvaṇṇitau thaumitau| ‘‘kalyāṇappaṭipattiyaṃ ṭhitaupī’’ti tīsukalyāṇappaṭipattīsu añ‍ñatarappaṭipattiyaṃ ṭhitaupi| ‘‘aṭṭhitaupī’’ti sabbappaṭipattibāhirau dussīlaupāpadhammauti adhippāyau| visaisatau pana vinayappaṭipatti aiva idha pariyattāti daṭṭhabbā| ‘‘sau’’ti saudussīlau pāpadhammau| attanaupi saraṇaṃ nahauti| avassaṃ apāyagāmīyaiva sau hautīti adhippāyau| ‘‘kutau saraṇaṃ bhavissatī’’ti yaujanā| ‘‘anaikaisu suttasahassaisū’’ti vinayaipi bahūni garahasuttapadāni dissanti suttantaisupi| visaisatau pana aggikkhandhaupamasuttādīsu|

saddhammavacanatthai| kilaisaisamainti vūpasamaintīti santauti vacanatthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘samitakilaisāna’’nti vuttaṃ| santa saddau pana pasatthaica, pūjitaica, sappurisaica, paṇḍitaica, dissatīti imaṃ abhidhānatthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘pasatthāna’’ntiādivuttaṃ| ‘‘sac‍cauvā dhammau saddhammau’’ti yaujanā| aitaina sabhāvatau atthisaṃvij‍jatīti santauti dassaiti| ‘‘sau’’ti añ‍ñatitthiya dhammau| ‘‘dhāraintassā’’ti savanuggahadhāraṇappaṭipaj‍janādivasaina dhāraintassa| ahitauyaiva sampaj‍jati, yaibhuyyaina duggati vipākattāti adhippāyau| ‘‘ayaṃ panā’’ti satthu saddhammau pana| ‘‘tathā dhāraintassā’’ti  ayaṃ mai hitauti dhāraintassa hitauyaivasampaj‍jati, sugati nibbāna sampāpakattāti adhippāyau|

‘‘samānadiṭṭhisīlāna’’nti samānadiṭṭhikānaṃ samānasīlānañ‍ca| aitaina samānadiṭṭhisīlā janā gaṇīyanti aitthāti gaṇau| saṃhanīyanti aikataukarīyanti aitthāti saṅghauti imamatthaṃ dīpaiti| saha aikatau dhammaṃ carantīti sahadhammikā| aikassa satthunau dhamma vinayai pabbajitā| taisaṃ sahadhammikānaṃ| bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau uttamagaṇau nāma| yai kaici laukai saṅghāvā gaṇāvā| tathāgatassa sāvakasaṅghau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyatīti hi vuttaṃ| sauyaiva idha gaṇuttamauti vuc‍cati visaisana para nipāta vasaināti adhippāyau| uttamasaddassa guṇanāmattā ‘‘guṇamhiyaiva pavattatī’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘tainā’’ti uttama saddaina| ‘‘gaṇauti saṅghauyaiva vuc‍cati’’ saṅghasaddassapi samūhaṭṭhai niruḷhattā, ‘‘sau vinayakammaisu pasiddhau’’ti pañ‍casaṅghā catuvaggasaṅghau, pañ‍cavaggasaṅghau, dasavaggasaṅghau, vīsativaggasaṅghau, atiraikavīsativaggasaṅghau, catuvaggakaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ, pañ‍cavaggakaraṇīyaṃ kammantiādinā pasiddhau pākaṭau| dakkhiṇā vuc‍cati kammañ‍ca kammaphalañ‍ca saddahitvā āyatiṃ vipākapphalappaṭi lābhatthāya din‍naṃ dānakammaṃ dakkhanti vaḍḍhanti sattā aitāyāti katvā| taṃdakkhiṇaṃ paṭiggaṇhituṃ arahatīti dakkhiṇaiyyau| ‘‘arahatī’’ti ca anuttara puñ‍ñakkhaitta visaisattādāyakaina icchita patthitassa āyatiṃ phalassa suṭṭhusampādanavasaina dāyakassa ca avirādhanatau asaṅkhyaiyyāppamaiyyavaḍḍhi āvahanatau ca paṭiggaṇhituṃ arahati| aivarūpaṃhidānaṃ nāma uḷāradānaṃ hauti| taṃ yaisudussīlaisudiyyati| taisaṃ khaitta duṭṭhattā dāyakaina icchitapatthitaṃ phalaṃ na sampādaiti| nipphalaṃ vā hauti| appapphalaṃ vā| aivaṃsati, tai dāyakañ‍ca virādhainti nāma| puñ‍ñapphalāni ca vināsainti nāma| sayañ‍ca taṃdānaṃ paṭiggahaṇatau vā paribhaugatau vā saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātanatau vā duggati bhāginau haunti| tasmātai aivarūpaṃ dānaṃ paṭiggaṇhituṃ nārahantīti| ‘‘upasampadākammaṃ sammuti nāmā’’ti aikaina pariyāyaina sammuti kammaṃ nāma| tañhi kāmaṃtaira sasusammuti kammaisunāgataṃ| ñatti catuttha kammavācā saṅkhātāya  pana saṅghasammutiyā siddhattā taina pariyāyaina sammuti kammanti vuc‍catīti| ‘‘upasampan‍nabhūmiṃ patvā’’ti upasampan‍nabhūmi saṅkhātaṃ upariṭhānantaraṃ patvā| tathā hi vuttaṃ vinayai bhikkhu vibhaṅgai| ñatti catutthaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaināti| tattha, ‘‘ṭhānārahainā’’ti upasampan‍na bhūmisaṅkhātaṃ ṭhānantaraṃ pāpaituṃ arahaināti atthau| ‘‘upasampan‍nabhūmī’’ti ca upasampan‍na sīlaṃ vuc‍cati| ‘‘vinayakammaisu pasiddhau’’ti| suṇātumaibhantai saṅghau tiādīsu pākaṭau| apica ‘‘sammuti saṅghau’’ti, daivasaṅghāsamāgatātiādīsu viya bahūnaṃ samūhanaṭṭhaina laukasammutiyā siddhausaṅghau sammuti saṅghautipi yuj‍jati| ‘‘ariyapuggalasamūhau’’ti puthuj‍janakalyāṇakau bhikkhu pubbai vuttanayaina sautāpatti maggaṭṭhai saṅgahitauti, taina saha aṭṭhavidhau ariya puggalasamūhau| ‘‘sammuti saṅghai antaugadhauyaivā’’ti aitaina dakkhiṇaiyyasaṅghau nāma visuṃ navattabbau| tasmiñ‍ca navattabbai sati, sammuti saṅghautipi vattabba kic‍caṃ natthi| bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau tvaiva vattabbaṃ hautīti dassaiti| sac‍camaitaṃ| idha pana saṅghavacanaina āgataṭṭhānassa duvidhattā saṅghassa duvidhatā vuttā| tattha sammuti saṅghassa āgataṭṭhānaṃ vinayakammaisūti vuttamaiva| idāni ariyasaṅghassa āgataṭṭhānaṃ dassaintau ‘‘tathāpī’’tiādimāha| tattha saraṇa gamana…pai॰… anussatiṭṭhānaisu upasampan‍nabhūtau bhikkhusaṅghauva gahaitabbau| dakkhiṇāvisuddhiṭṭhānai pana puggalikadānaisu catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau upāsakaupi sāmaṇairaupi yuj‍jati| saṅghika dānaisu pana bhikkhusaṅghauva| ‘‘tathā tathā saṃvaṇṇaitvā’’ti āhunaiyyau, pāhunaiyyau, dakkhiṇaiyyau, añ‍jalīkaraṇīyyau, anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā, tiādināsuṭṭhu thaumaitvā| sau dakkhiṇaiyya saṅghau| ‘‘puthuj‍jana saṅghau’’ti idaṃ ariya saṅghaina vinākaivalaṃ puthuj‍janasaṅghaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| aittha ca saṅgha vacanaina saṅgha pariyāpan‍nau aikaupi bhikkhu gahaitabbau| sau sacai puthuj‍janau hauti, anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ nahauti| yadi ariya puggalau hauti, anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ hauti|

dakkhiṇavibhaṅgasuttai  saṅghikadānai bhavissanti khau panānanda gautrabhunau kāsāvakaṇṭhā dussīlā pāpadhammā| taisaṃpi saṅghaṃ uddissa dānaṃ dassanti| tadāpānanda saṅghagataṃ dakkhiṇaṃ asaṅkhyaiyyaṃ appamaiyyanti vadāmīti vuttattā saṅghikaṭṭhānaṃ patvā kauci bhikkhu dakkhiṇaiyya saṅghai asaṅgahitauti natthīti daṭṭhabbau|

khaittaṃ dūsaintīti khaittaduṭṭhāni| tiṇāni| khaittaduṭṭhā kilaisā| vuttañhidhammapadai|

tiṇadausāni khaittāni, rāgadausā ayaṃpajā|

tasmāhi vītarāgaisu, din‍naṃ hauti mahapphalaṃ||

tiṇadausāni khaittāni, dausadausā ayaṃpajā|

tasmāhi vītadausaisu, din‍naṃ hauti mahapphalaṃ||

tiṇadausāni khaittāni, mauhadausā ayaṃpajā|

tasmāhi vītamauhaisu, din‍naṃ hauti mahapphalanti||

idha pana sātissayatau khaittaduṭṭhai kilaisai dassaituṃ ‘‘sak‍kāyadiṭṭhivicikicchānusayāna’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘sabbhāvā’’ti santassa vij‍jamānassabhāvau sabbhāvautiviggahau| ‘‘assā’’ti puthuj‍jana saṅghassa| ‘‘saddhammapadai vuttanayainā’’ti apicayadaggaināti vuttanayaina|

sasaddhammagaṇuttamapadatthānudīpanī niṭṭhitā|

8. ‘‘abhivādiyā’’ti sukhīhauhisappurisāti aivaṃ abhivadāpaitvā| abhivādanañ‍ca nāma vandanāmaivāti vuttaṃ ‘‘vanditvā’’ti| vandantauhi vandanaiyyai vuddhai tathā vadāpaiti nāma| tathā vadanañ‍ca vandanaiyyānaṃvuddhānaṃ vattaṃ| ‘‘pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaitvā’’ti paṭimukhaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā| ‘‘tividhā’’ti dvāra bhaidaina tividhā| ‘‘vandanaiyyāna’’nti buddhādīnaṃ| ‘‘nipaj‍jantau’’ti nipatantau| ‘‘avandiyaisū’’ti bhikkhūhi avanditabbai sunavakatarādīsu| ‘‘guṇapadānī’’ti guṇadīpakāni arahaṃ sammāsambuddhautiādipadāni| aitthacakāyainavandatītiādi upacāra vacanaṃ hauti| yathā cakkhunā rūpaṃ passatīti| tathāhi vuttaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu  cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvāti karaṇavasaina cakkhūti laddhavauhāraina rūpadassana samatthaina cakkhu viñ‍ñāṇaina rūpaṃ disvā| paurāṇā panāhu cakkhu rūpaṃ na passati, acittattā| cittaṃ na passati, acakkhuttā| dvārārammaṇa saṅghaṭṭanai pana sati cakkhu pasādavatthu kainacittaina passati| īdisī panaisā kathā dhanunā vijjhatītiādīsu viya sasambhāra kathānāma hauti| tasmā cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvāti cakkhu viñ‍ñāṇaina rūpaṃ disvāti ayamaivaittha atthauti| aitthahi ‘‘kāyainā’’ti kāya viñ‍ñattisaṅkhātaṃ dvāra rūpaṃ karaṇaṃ hauti| tasmā cakkhunāti vacanai upacāra vacanai sati kāyaināti vacanaṃpi upacāra vacananti viñ‍ñāyati| tathā cakkhunāti padai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaināti atthai sati kāyaināti padaipi kāyakammaināti atthau viñ‍ñāyati| vācāya vandati, manasāvandatī,ti padaisupi aisanayau| aivañ‍ca sati, kāya kammainavandāmi, vacīkammaina vandāmi, manaukammaina vandāmi, tīhi kammaihi vandāmīti idamaiva mukhyavacananti siddhaṃ hauti| sabbamidaṃ indriya saṃvarasīlaṭṭhānai aṭṭhakathāsu āgatattā vuttaṃ| cakkhunāti idaṃ pana mukhya karaṇa vacanamaiva sambhavati| kasmā, cakkhussa dassanasaṅkhātassa cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu puraijātindriyapac‍cayavisaisattā| sautaina saddaṃsutvātiādīsupi aisanayau|

kāyainavandati, vācāyavandatī,ti aittha taṃkāyavacīviñ‍ñatti rūpa dvayaṃ kiñ‍cāpi sahajāta caitanā kammaina jātaṃ hauti| na taṃ caitanākammaṃ viñ‍ñatti dvayainajātaṃ| aivaṃ santaipi taṃ rūpa dvayaṃ tassācaitanāya kāyavandanākamma, vacīvandanākamma siddhiyā upanissaya pac‍cayavisaisau hauti| yathātaṃ mātitau jātau puttau vuddhi pattau taṃ taṃ kammaisu mātuyā bala vūpanissayau hauti| aivañ‍ca katvā taṃ rūpadvayaṃ abhidhammai dvārarūpanti vuttaṃ| tasmā ‘kāyaina, vācāyā,ti idaṃpi mukhya karaṇa vacanamaivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| kāyaināti pana kāyakammaināti atthai sati, tassakammassa vandanākiriyāya saha abhaidau āpaj‍jatīti cai| nāpaj‍jati| kasmā caitanāhaṃ bhikkhavai kammaṃ vadāmi, caitayitvā kammaṃ karauti ‘kāyaina, vācāya, manasāti imasmiṃ suttai yathāhi ‘‘caitayitvā’’ti purimacaitanāhi caita yitvā| ‘‘kammaṃ karautī’’ti pacchimaṃ san‍niṭṭhāpana caitanā kammaṃ karautīti atthau| tathā idhapi purimapacchimacaitanā sambhavatauti| aittha hi ‘‘kāyakammainā’’ti purimacaitanā kammaṃ gayhati| ‘‘vandatī’’ti pacchima san‍niṭṭhāpanacaitanākammanti|

9. ‘‘racayantau’’ti vidahantau| ‘‘racayissatī’’ti apac‍cakkhai atītai anāgatavacanaṃ| ‘‘pautthakāruḷha’’nti pautthaka pattaisulikhanavasaina āruḷhaṃ|

10. abhidhammatthapadai| ‘‘abhidhammai’’ti abhidhammappakaraṇai| ‘‘aittha, aitainā’’ti vacanaihi saṅgahasaddassa aikasaisavidhānaṃpi viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘añ‍ñaṃpāḷidvayaṃ vuc‍cati’’| kasmātaṃ pāḷidvayaṃ abhidhammau nāmāti vuttaṃ ‘‘tañ‍cā’’tiādi| ‘‘yathāpavattai’’ti attanau pac‍cayānurūpaṃ pavattai| ‘‘paramatthadhammai aivā’’ti dvai mai bhikkhavai puggalā, tayaumaibhikkhavai puggalātiādinā pañ‍ñattivauhāraina pavattāpi daisanā paramattha dhammaihi vinā napavattati| paramattha dhammānaṃ nānattavasainaiva puggalānaṃ nānattasambhavatau| tasmā paramatthadhammai aiva dīpaiti| na āṇāvidhānaṃ dīpaiti| ‘‘dvīsudhammaisū’’ti niddhāraṇai bhummavacanaṃ| ‘‘yauitaratau’’ti niddhāraṇīyaṃ| ‘‘yau’’ti yau dhammau| ‘‘itaratau’’ti itara dhammatau suttanta dhammatau| ‘‘aivañ‍ca katvā’’ti iminākāraṇaināti atthau| aṭṭhakathāsu vuttanti sambandhau| daisaitabbappakārānaṃ anavasaisavibhattivasaina atiraikatā, suddha dhammādhiṭṭhāna daisanā pavattivasaina visaisatā yaujaitabbā| ‘‘yatau’’ti yasmā anavasaisavibhattitau, ativitthāradaisanābhāvatauti vuttaṃ hauti| kasmā daivaisu aiva daisaintīti āha ‘‘na hi manussā’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘na hi manussā paṭiggahaituṃ sak‍kauntī’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘pavattanayaugya’’nti pavattanatthāya pahauntaṃ| ‘‘kathāmagga’’nti daisanākathāpabandhaṃ| ‘‘aikamātikānu bandhā’’ti kusalā dhammā akusalā dhammātiādikaṃ aikaṃ abhidhamma mātikaṃ anugatā| ‘‘tassā’’ti abhidhammassa| atiraika visaisatanti sambandhau| ‘‘tatthā’’ti tissaṃ aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ| ādimhiyaivatattha kainaṭṭhaina abhidhammau, dhammātiraika dhammavisaisaṭṭhai nā-ti  vatvā tadatthaṃ vitthāraintau suttañhi patvā pañ‍cakkhandhā aikadaisainaiva vibhattā, nanippadaisaina| abhidhammaṃ patvā pana nippadaisatauva vibhattā-ti vuttaṃ| taināha ‘‘dhammanāmikāna’’ntiādiṃ| ‘‘dhammau panā’’ti pāḷidvayamāha|‘‘aivaṃ santaipī’’ti aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ aivaṃ vicāritaipisati| ‘‘sabbajaiṭṭhakau’’ti tiṇṇaṃ piṭakānaṃ majjhaisabbajaiṭṭhakau| kasmā sabbajaiṭṭhakau siyāti āha ‘‘vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaintassahī’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘vivaṇṇaintassā’’ti garahantassa| carati pavattatīti cak‍kaṃ| laukasmiṃ kainaci samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā paṭinivattituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ āṇāvidhānaṃ āṇācak‍kaṃ nāma| tathā asak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ daisanā vidhānaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ nāma| tattha, yau pana bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsau-tiādinā nayaina pavattaṃ āṇāvidhānaṃ āṇācak‍kaṃ nāma| cattārimāni bhikkhavai ariyasac‍cānī-tiādikaṃ daisanāvidhānaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ nāma| tadubhayaṃpi kauci bhindituṃ pārājikaṃ vā bhikkhuṃ apārājikaṃ kātuṃ catussac‍caṃ vā dhammaṃ asac‍caṃ kātuṃ nasak‍kauti| atha khau karauntauyaiva daivadattauviya āpāyikau hauti| pārājikauca bhikkhu attānaṃ suddhaṃ akarauntau apāyagāmīyaiva hauti| aivaṃ sabbaisu vinaya sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ kainaci paṭinivattituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyattā tadubhayaṃpi appaṭivattiyaṃ cak‍kaṃ nāma hauti| ‘‘vinayau nāma sāsanassa mūla’’nti vinayai ṭhitai bhikkhusaṅghau pañ‍ñāyati| bhikkhu saṅghai pañ‍ñāyantai tividhaupisaddhammaupañ‍ñāyati, tividhaṃpi satthusāsanaṃ tiṭṭhati| aivaṃ vinayau tividhassasāsanassa mūlaṃ hautīti| kathañ‍capariyattisaddhammai pañ‍ñāyantai tividhaṃpi sāsanaṃ tiṭṭhatīti| vuc‍catai| tattha sāsanaṃ tiṭṭhatīti kittakaṃkālaṃ tiṭṭhatīti| pañ‍cavassasahassāni tiṭṭhatīti paurāṇaṭṭhakathāsukathayiṃsu| micchāvādinaupanavadanti vinayai cūḷavaggaibhikkhunikkhandhakai sacai ānanda mamasāsanaimātugāmau pabbaj‍jaṃ nalabhaiyya| vassasahassaṃ saddhammau tiṭṭhaiyya| idāni mātugāmassa pabbaj‍jā anuñ‍ñātā gautamiyā punappunaṃ āyācanaṃ upādāya| pañ‍caivadāni ānanda vassasatāni saddhammauṭhassatīti vuttaṃ| tasmā buddhasāsanaṃ  pañ‍cavassasatāni aiva tiṭṭhati| tatauparaṃ aikadivasaṃpi na tiṭṭhati| idāni sāsanappaṭi rūpakamattaṃ hautīti| taṃ taisaṃ micchā vacanamattaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍caivavassasatānī’’ti idaṃ pana san‍niṭṭhāna vacanaṃ nahauti| mātugāmānaṃ ādīnava dīpanamattavacanaṃ| sau ca ādīnavau aṭṭhagarudhammai saṇṭhapaitvā satthārā aiva paṭibāhitau| puna ‘‘vassasahassa’’nti idamaivasan‍niṭṭhāna vacanaṃ jātanti| aitthapikaicivadanti vassasahassamaiva sāsanaṃ tiṭṭhati, tatauparaṃ aikadivasa mattaṃpi na tiṭṭhati, antaradhāyati| tadā sīmāyaupi asīmā haunti| pacchātāsu upasampāditāpi anupasampan‍nā haunti| idāni sāsanappaṭi rūpakamattaṃ hautīti| idaṃpi taisaṃ atthañ‍cakāraṇañ‍ca adisvā ajānitvā vuttattā micchā vacanamattaṃ hauti| ayaṃ panaittha atthau| ‘‘vassasahassaṃ saddhammau tiṭṭhaiyyā’’ti vassasahassamaiva saddhammau aparihāyamānau tiṭṭhaiyya| tatauparaṃ pana na tiṭṭhaiyya, anuk‍kamaina parihāyamānau gacchaiyyāti| kathaṃ pana aparihāyamānau tiṭṭhati, kathañ‍ca parihāyamānau gacchatīti| vuc‍catai| pañ‍casaṅghā vaiditabbā| yaisu saṅghai susaddhammau tiṭṭhati| katamai pañ‍ca| khīṇāsavasaṅghau, anāgāmisaṅghau, sakadāgāmisaṅghau, sautāpan‍nasaṅghau, puthuj‍janakalyāṇakasaṅghau,ti| tattha, vassasahassabbhantarai sabbaipañ‍casaṅghā pañ‍ñāyanti| aivaṃ vassasahassaṃ saddhammau aparihāyamānau tiṭṭhati| tatauparaṃ khīṇāsavasaṅghau na pañ‍ñāyati| saisāni cattārivassasahassāni anuk‍kamaina saisānaṃ catun‍naṃ saṅghānaṃ khaittāni jātāni| aivaṃ tatauparaṃ parihāyamānau gacchatīti ayamaittha atthau| kāraṇaṃ vuc‍catai| ‘‘saddhammau tiṭṭhaiyyā’’ti aittha tividhau saddhammau ‘pariyattisaddhammau, paṭipattisaddhammau, paṭivaidhasaddhammau,ti| sau aiva tividhaṃ sāsananti ca vuc‍cati| tattha, pariyatti saddhammau nāma sāṭṭhakathānitīṇipiṭakāni| sauca aitarahi paripuṇṇau tiṭṭhati| kathaṃ pariyatti sāsanaṃ paṭirūpakamattaṃ bhavaiyya| bhikkhū ca pariyattikammikā anaika satasahassamattā pañ‍ñāyanti| kathañ‍cidaṃ sāsanaṃ tatauparaṃ aikadivasaṃpi na tiṭṭhaiyya| tai ca bhikkhū sīlappaṭipattiyaṃ ṭhitā anaika satasahassamattā aitarahi sandhissanti| kathañ‍ca paṭipatti sāsanaṃ tatauparaṃ  na tiṭṭhaiyya| pariyattiyāca paṭipattiyāca tiṭṭhamānāyapaṭivaidhasaddhammaupi na tiṭṭhatīti na vattabbau| yathāhi-aikau dhanasaiṭṭhi nāma atthi| sau puttadhītu paramparānaṃ atthāya mahantāratananidhayau bhūmiyaṃ bahūsuṭṭhānaisu nidahitvā ṭhapitā haunti| pautthakaisu ca taisaṃ pavattiṃ paripuṇṇaṃ likhitvā ṭhapaiti| tattha nimīsu ca pautthakaisu ca akkharaisu ca dharantaisu tainidhayau nassanti antaradhāyantīti na vattabbāyaiva| aivamidaṃ sāsanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| tatthahi nidhīnaṃnidhānabhūmisadisaṃ taipiṭakaṃ buddhavacanaṃ, dhanaratanasadisāni dhammaratanāni| yathā ca saiṭṭhivaṃsaijātau balasampan‍nau purisau pautthakaṃ passitvā suṭṭhu khaṇantau tāniratanāni labhissatiyaiva| aivamidhapi balasampan‍nau bhikkhu daisanādhammaṃ sutvā suṭṭhu paṭipaj‍jantau tānidhammaratanāni labhissatiyaiva| labhamānai ca sati, kathaṃ tānidhammaratanāni antarahitāni| sīmānañ‍ca pavattivānivatti vā āṇācak‍kassaiva visayau hauti| na dhammacak‍kassa| yañ‍ca vuttaṃ-pañ‍caivavassasatānisaddhammau ṭhassatīti ca, - vassasahassaṃ saddhammau tiṭṭhaiyyāti ca| idañ‍ca vacanaṃ dhammacak‍kamaiva hauti, na āṇācak‍kaṃ| baddhasīmāyau ca nivattamānā dvīhi kāraṇaihi nivattanti antaradhāyanti| āṇācak‍kabhūtāya kammavācāya samūhananaina vā, sāsanassa vā antaradhānaina| tattha vassasahassapariyantai kammavācāya samūhananañ‍ca natthi| sāsanantaradhānañ‍ca nāma anāgatai dhātuparinibbānaina paricchin‍naṃ hauti| dhātuparinibbānaihi jātai sabbaṃ āṇācak‍kaṃ vigataṃ hauti, antaradhāyati| dhammacak‍kaṃ pana daivalaukaisu yāvānāgatabuddhakālāpi pavattissatiyaiva āḷavakapucchāgāthāyau viya| apicayau vau ānanda mayā dhammau ca vinayau ca daisitau pañ‍ñattau| sau vau mamac‍cayaina satthā-ti vuttaṃ| saucati piṭakabhūtau dhammavinayau satthā aitarahi tividhaṃpi sāsanaṃ laukassa dīpaintau pakāsaintau tiṭṭhati| yataubuddhabhāsikānaṃ daivamanussānaṃ nānā bāhirakaihi janaihi asādhāraṇau mahantau ñāṇālaukau aitarahi vij‍jautamānau pavattati| taihi sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina daisitāni aitarahidharamānāni caturāsīti dhammakkhandha sahassāni sutvā anamataggaisaṃsārai  anantāsu laukadhātūsu sabbaṃlaukattayappavattiñ‍ca, sabbaṃdhammappavattiñ‍ca, sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇānugatikaina sutamayañ‍ñāṇaina jānanti| āyatiñ‍ca saggatthāya ca maggaphalanibbānatthāya ca nānāpuñ‍ñakiriyavatthūni ārabhanti, pabbajanti| pariyattiṃ pariyāpuṇanti| paṭipattiṃ pūrainti| bhāvanaṃbhāvainti| idaṃ sabbaṃ sāsanappaṭirūpakamattaṃna hauti| aikanta sāsanaṃ hauti| kasmā, yathā dhammaṃ yathā vinayaṃ paṭipaj‍janatau| imāya ca paṭipattiyā āyatiṃ saggamaggaphala nibbānappaṭilābhāya saṃvattanikattāti| aittāvatā sabbaṃ micchāvādīnaṃ micchāvacanaṃ vidhamitaṃviddhaṃsitaṃ hautīti|

paṇāmagāthāvaṇṇanā|

11. dutīyagāthāvaṇṇanāyaṃ| ‘‘ādigāthāyā’’ti pathamagāthāvākyaina| ‘‘taṃ taṃ payaujanasahitai’’ti tainatainapayaujanaina sahitai| ‘‘pañ‍ca atthai’’ti pañ‍capiṇḍatthai| ‘‘taiabhidhammatthai’’ti abhidhammatthapadai dīpitai taiabhidhammatthai| ‘‘tatthā’’ti tissaṃ dutīyagāthāyaṃ| ‘‘natu vutta’’nti napanapakaraṇaṃpi pubbaivuttaṃ hauti| abhidhammatthā kutaupubbaivuttā hauntīti yaujanā| aivaṃtīsu atthavikappaisu pathamassa kālaviraudhaṃ dassaitvā idāni dutīya tatīyānaṃ saddatauviraudhaṃ vattuṃ ‘‘nacā’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘ādimhi yaivā’’ti aitaina yadākadāci pac‍cāmasanaṃ appadhānanti dassaiti| ‘‘appadhānapadānī’’ti abhidhammatthasaṅgahanti imasmiṃ aikasmiṃ samāsapadai purimāni visaisana padāni| tattha sabbathāpi vuttāti yaujitai sati, tasmiṃ abhidhammatthasaṅgahappakaraṇai tasmiṃ abhidhammattha padaivā sabbathā mayā vuttāti atthau hauti| sau na yuj‍jati| tasmiṃ abhidhammai buddhaina bhagavatā sabbathā vuttāti atthau yuj‍jati| appadhānapadaṃ pac‍cāmasatīti dausaupana āpaj‍jataiva| taināha ‘‘aivañhisatī’’tiādiṃ| tattha, ‘‘aivañhisatī’’ti aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ viya idha abhidhammatthasaṅgahapadaṃ pac‍cāmasantai sati| hisaddau phala vākyajautakau| laddhaguṇajautakautipi yuj‍jati| ‘‘paṭikkhittā hauti’’  tasmiṃ abhidhammatthasaṅgahapadai mayā sabbathā vuttāti atthassa sambhavatau| na kaivalaṃ sau aiva dausau āpaj‍jati| aparaupidausau atthīti dassaituṃ ‘‘sāhī’’tiādimāha| dhātukathāyaṃ vuttainasaṅgahāsaṅgahādippakārainātiyaujanā|

12. paramatthapadavaṇṇanāyaṃ| visaisanapadaṃ nāma katthaci bhūtakathanatthāyavā payuj‍jati kaṇhaukākau, saitaubakau,ti| katthaci añ‍ñanivattanatthāya vā payuj‍jati nīlaupaṭau, nīlaṃpuppha, nti| idha pana añ‍ña nivattanatthāyāti dassaituṃ ‘‘duvidhānihisac‍cānī’’tiādimāha| pañ‍ñāpīyatītipañ‍ñatti| pañ‍ñāpanañ‍ca nāma samaggānaṃ janānaṃ vauhāraina ca sampaṭicchanaina cāti dvīhi aṅgaihi sijjhatīti āha ‘‘taicamahājanā’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘tasmātai sammuti sac‍canti vuc‍cantī’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘sammatattā’’ti vauharitattācaiva sampaṭicchitattā ca| ‘‘vacīsac‍caviratisac‍cāna’’nti aittha vacīsac‍caṃ nāma musāvādarahitaṃ sac‍cavacanaṃ| viratisac‍caṃ nāma sammāvācāvirati| sāhi musāvādādīhi vacīduc‍caritaihi viramaṇamattaina vacīsac‍canti vuc‍cati| ‘‘vatthubhūtattā’’ti adhiṭṭhānabhūtattā| sammutisac‍canti vuc‍canti, sammatattā sammuti ca, sā sac‍cānaṃ vatthubhūtattā sac‍cañ‍cāti katvā| sammutiyā siddhaṃ sac‍caṃ sammutisac‍cantipi yuj‍jati| ‘‘sammāpaṭipaj‍jantā’’ti pāṇau nahantabbau, sabbaisattā avairā hauntūtiādinā sammāpaṭipaj‍jantā| ‘‘sabbalaukiyasampattiyau’’ti dānasīlādīnaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūnaṃ phalavipākabhūtā sabbalaukiyasampattiyau| sabbai ‘‘baudhisambhāradhammai’’ti dānapāramisīlapāramiādikaipāramidhammai| ‘‘ārādhaintī’’ti sampādainti| ‘‘micchāpaṭipaj‍jantā’’ti duc‍carita durājīvamicchājīvādīnaṃ vasaina micchāpaṭipaj‍jantā| ‘‘aivaṃ mahantaṃ sammuti sac‍ca’’nti aitaina ahaṃ paramattha sac‍camaivagaṇhāmīti sammuti sac‍caṃ nabhinditabbaṃ| bhindantauhi sabbasampattīhi paribāhirau assāti dassaiti| kathañ‍ca taṃ bhindatīti| sattau nāma natthi| sattassa bhavatausaṅkanti nāma natthi| bhavanibbattakaṃ kusalākusalakammaṃ nāma natthīti gaṇhantau ucchaidadiṭṭhiyaṃ tiṭṭhati| sabbasampattīhi paribāhirau hauti| apāya pūrakau bhavatīti| ‘‘vij‍jamānantvaiva gaṇhāpaitī’’ti sañ‍ñā cittadiṭṭhi  vipal‍lāsānaṃ vatthubhāvaina gaṇhāpaiti| taināha ‘‘sak‍kāyadiṭṭhī’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ viparītañhi sammutisac‍ca’’nti aitainasammuti sac‍camaivadaḷhaṃ gahaitvā paramattha sac‍caṃ nabhinditabbaṃ| bhindantauhi tāhi diṭṭhīhi namuc‍cati| kathañ‍ca taṃ bhindati| khandhai vā khandhamuttakaivā attajīvai gahaitvā taica attajīvā parammaraṇā ucchij‍jantīti gaṇhantau ucchaidadiṭṭhiyaṃ tiṭṭhati| tai ca attajīvā bhavābhavaisusassatā hutvā bhavataubhavaṃ saṃsaranti sandhāvantīti gaṇhantausassatadiṭṭhiyaṃ tiṭṭhati| ‘‘navisaṃvādaintī’’ti viparītaṃ nāpādainti| ‘‘taṃ panā’’ti sabhāvasac‍caṃ pana| anubhavanabhaidamattaṃ upādāyaiva vaidanā sukhāti vuttā| sabbākāratau sukhabhūtattā vaidanā sukhāti vuttā nahauti| ‘‘sabbāpivaidanā dukkhā aivā’’ti padhānatthau| tattha anubhavanabhaidau tividhau| sātatau vā anubhavanaṃ, assātatau vā, majjhattatau vā| ‘‘dukkhā aivā’’ti bhayaṭṭhaina dukkhā aiva| bhayaṭṭhaināti ca saṃsāra bhayadassīhibhāyitabbaṭṭhaina| sukhau vipākau yaisaṃ tai sukhavipākā| taibhūmakakusalā| ‘‘kusalasammatā’’ti aitaina sabhāvasac‍caipi aitaivauhārā laukasammuti nissitāti dīpaiti| ‘‘sāsavatā’’ti āsavaihi sahitabhāvau| ‘‘saṃkilaisi katā’’ti saṃkilaisa dhammaihi saṃyuttabhāvau| aughaihi ca yaugaihi ca upādānaihi ca pattabbabhāvau ‘‘aughanīyayauganīya upādānīyatā’’| adhikā attā ajjhattā| bahiddhārukkhairūpadhammārukkhassa attānāma sāraṭṭhaina| sākhāyaṃ rūpadhammā sākhāya attānāma sāraṭṭhaina| sattasantānapariyāpan‍nā pana rūpārūpadhammā taṇhāpariggaha daḷhaṭṭhaina tatau bahiddhā attatau adhikā attāti atthaina ajjhattāti laukasammuti hauti| taināha ‘‘ajjhattatikañ‍cā’’tiādiṃ| dukkhaniraudha maggabhāvau ca, iti idaṃ catuk‍kaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ nāmāti yaujanā| ‘‘idamaivā’’ti idaṃ catuk‍kamaiva| ‘‘acalamāna’’nti aitaina ariyasaddassa atthaṃ dīpaiti| taibhūmaka dhammānaṃ sukhatā nāma calā hauti| kasmā, anic‍ca dhammattā| tai dhammaisukhāti gahaitvā attanau ajjhattaṅgaṃ karauntā acirainaiva dukkhaṃ pāpuṇanti| tai dhammai dukkhāti ñatvā taihivimuttā puna dukkhaṃ pāpuṇantīti natthi| aisanayau saisaariyasac‍caisu| ‘‘taisū’’tiādimhi duvidhānihi sac‍cānīti vuttaisu dvīsu sac‍caisu| ‘‘taina vutta’’ntiādi laddhaguṇavacanaṃ| ‘‘yau vinā añ‍ñāpadaisainā’’ti aittha añ‍ñāpadaisau nāma aṭṭhadhamma samaudhānaṃ nissāya ghaṭasaṇṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, paṭasaṇṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, taṃ saṇṭhānaṃ attanau sabhāvaina vinā añ‍ñāpadaisaina siddhaṃ hauti| yāpanacintana kiriyā nāma atthi| yaṃ cittanti vuc‍cati| sā añ‍ñāpadaisaina siddhā na hauti| attanau sabhāvainaivasiddhā| aisanayau phusanakiriyā, vaidayitakiriyā, dīsūti| imamatthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘yauvinā añ‍ñāpadaisainā’’tiādimāha| ‘‘cittainaparikappaitvā’’ti manauviñ‍ñāṇa cittaina avij‍jamānaṃ saṇṭhānaṃ vij‍jamānaṃ katvā| ‘‘saviggahaṃ katvā’’ti sarīraṃ katvā| vatthu dabbasahitaṃ katvāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘cittamayaucittanimmitau’’ti supinantai diṭṭharūpāni viya cittainapakatau cittaina nimmitau| kasmā sabhāvasiddhau paramatthau nāmāti āha ‘‘sauhī’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘santī’’ti aitaina asadhātu vasaina atthauti siddhaṃ vuttaṃ| saddābuddhīhi araṇīyatau upagantabbatau atthautipivadanti| ‘‘itaratau’’ti parikappasiddhatau| ‘‘paramau’’ti adhikau| taināha ‘‘uk‍kaṃsagatau’’ti| aitaina paramasaddassa adhikatthaṃ vadati| idāni tassa uttamatthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘apicā’’tiādivuttaṃ| tattha, imasmiṃ buddhasāsanai pañ‍casāsana kic‍cāni mahantāni abhiñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ dhammānaṃ abhijānanaṃ| pariñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ parijānanaṃ| pahātabbānaṃ pahānaṃ| sacchi kātabbānaṃ sacchikaraṇaṃ| bhāvaitabbānaṃ bhāvanāti| tattha sabbaipi paramattha dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā nāma| dukkha sac‍cadhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā nāma| samudaya sac‍cadhammā pahātabbā nāma| sāmañ‍ñapphalāni ca nibbānañ‍ca sacchikātabbā nāma| maggasac‍cadhammā bhāvaitabbā nāma| taisu dhammaisu taisaṃ kic‍cānaṃ siddhiyā imasmiṃ sāsanai sāsanakic‍caṃ siddhaṃ hauti| niṭṭhānaṃ gacchati| tai ca dhammā aiva rūpānaṃ sāsana kic‍cānaṃ avirādhakattā avisaṃvādakattā uttamaṭṭhaina paramatthā nāma hauntīti imamatthaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘apicā’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘yai’’tiyaijanā| ‘‘aya’’nti ayaṃ dhammau| ‘‘tassā’’ti tassaabhiñ‍ñaiyyassa, tassapariñ‍ñaiyyassa, tassapahātabbassa, tassasacchikā tabbassa  tassabhāvaitabbassāti sambandhau| paramatthavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

‘‘taṃ nasundara’’nti byañ‍janatau nasundaraṃ| nakaivalaṃ byañ‍janatauyaiva nasundaraṃ, atthataupi nasundaramaiva| catusac‍ca dhammāhi pac‍caikabuddhañ‍ñāṇassapi gaucarā haunti| pañ‍cañaiyya dhammā pana sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassaiva| tattha catusac‍ca dhammā nāma paramattha dhammā aiva| pañ‍cañaiyyadhammā pana sabba pañ‍ñattiyā saha sabbaparamattha dhammā| sabbañ‍ñubuddhānaṃ catusac‍cābhi sambaudhau dhamma pañ‍ñattiyā saha sijjhati| pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ catusac‍ca sambaudhau dhammapañ‍ñattiyā saha nasijjhati| tasmā tai sayaṃ paṭividdhaṃ catusac‍ca dhammaṃ nāma pañ‍ñattiṃ nīharitvā paraisaṃ daisaituṃ na sak‍kaunti| taisaṃ catusac‍casambaudhau mūgassa supinadassanaṃ viya hautīti aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ| tasmā pañ‍ñattiyā saha pañ‍ca ñaiyya dhammā aiva sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa gaucarāti sak‍kāvattunti|

paramatthapadavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

13. cittavacanatthai| nahi sā ārammaṇainavinā labbhati| cintaitīti vuttai kiṃ cintaiti, ārammaṇaṃ cintaitīti aivaṃ ārammaṇabhūtaina kammapadaina vinā asambhavatau| tasmā idha cintanāti daṭṭhabbā, tasmā assa nāmaṃ siddhanti daṭṭhabbanti sambandhau| sutamayañ‍ñāṇaṃ, cintāmayañ‍ñāṇa, nti aittha ārammaṇassa bhūtasabhāva cintāpi atthi, sāpañ‍ñāaivāti taṃ nivattaituṃ ‘‘ārammaṇa…pai॰… ṇūpaladdhiyaivā’’ti vuttaṃ| cittaṃ, manau, mānasaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇa, nti sabbaṃ cittassa nāmaṃ| ārammaṇa pac‍cayappaṭibaddhaṃ hauti| na añ‍ñapac‍cayappaṭibaddhaṃ| na ca añ‍ñapac‍cayaina laddhaṃ nāmaṃ| aivarūpassa ārammaṇa vijānana saṅkhātassa atthantarassabaudhakaṃ nahautīti dassaituṃ ‘‘santaisu cā’’tiādivuttaṃ| ‘‘aitainā’’ti idaṃ kattunau kiriyāsādhanai atissayūpakārakaṃ karaṇa sādhanaṃ vadatīti dassaituṃ ‘‘tañhī’’tiādivuttaṃ| ‘‘cintanamatta’’nti aittha mattasaddau visaisanivatti atthauti, taina nivattitaṃ atthaṃ dassaiti ‘‘sabbaipihī’’tiādinā| ‘‘viggahau vā’’ti sarīraṃ vā| pac‍cayaina  āyattā pac‍cayāyattā| ‘‘āyattā’’ti sambandhā| vattanaṃ vutti| uppaj‍janaṃ vā ṭhiti vā| pac‍cayāyattā vutti aitaisanti ‘‘pac‍cayāyatta vuttinau’’| ‘‘thāmainā’’tiādi añ‍ñamañ‍ñavaivacanāni| ‘‘aikaṃ bhāvasādhanamaiva padhānatau labbhatī’’ti idaṃ dhammānaṃ taṃ taṃ kiriyā mattabhāvaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| kiriyāmattabhūtāpi pana tai dhammāsayaṃ nānāpac‍cayā vatthāyaṃ ṭhitā vā haunti nānāpac‍cayuppan‍nāvatthāyaṃ ṭhitā vā| tasmā paramattha padaisupi yathārahaṃ tadañ‍ñasādhanānaṃ paṭilābhau avāritau hauti| itarathā haitu pac‍cayau, ārammaṇa pac‍cayau, sahajātapac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayautiādīsu kathaṃ bhāvasādhanaṃ yuttaṃ siyāti| ‘‘padhānatau’’ti mukhyatau| ‘‘abhaidassa cintanassabhaidakaraṇa’’nti idaṃ cintaitīti cittantikataṃ kattusādhanaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| ‘‘silāputtakassā’’ti bhaisaj‍jamūlānaṃ pisanasilāpautakassa| tassa sarīraṃ nāma visuṃ aṅgaṃ natthi| abhin‍naṃpi bhin‍naṃ katvā vuc‍cati ‘‘silāputtakasarīra’’nti| idaṃ abhaidassabhaidakaraṇaṃ nāma abhūtarūpaṃ hauti| payaujanai sati vattabbaṃ, asati na vattabbanti āha ‘‘tathākaraṇañ‍cā’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘paraparikappitassā’’ti paraihi añ‍ñatitthā cariyaihi paricintitassa| ‘‘satihi…pai॰… kappanāyā’’ti sacai attā atthi, attā cintaiti, tasmā attā cittaunāmātiādi vattabbaṃ| na vattabbaṃ cintaitīti cittanti, cittassa kiriyāmattattā| na pana attādikau kattā nāma atthi| tasmā kiriyā mattamaiva kattāraṃ katvā ‘‘cintaitīti citta’’nti vuttaṃ| taina viñ‍ñāyatilaukai attādikaukattā nāma natthīti| idaṃ abhaidassabhaida parikappanāya payaujananti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘attappadhānau’’ti kiriyāsādhanai bahūnaṃkārakānaṃ majjhai sayaṃpadhānau sayaṃjaiṭṭhakau hutvā| ‘‘taṃkattubhāva’’nti laukaisiddhaṃ kattubhāvaṃ| ‘‘puna karaṇabhāva’’nti puna laukai siddhaṃ karaṇabhāvaṃ| aivaṃ cittassa vacanatthaṃ dassaitvā idāni tassa abhidhānatthaṃ dassaintau ‘‘apicaitthā’’tiādimāha| yathayidaṃ yai imai tiracchānagatā pāṇā cittāvicittā| aivaṃ cittaṃ vicittaṃ yaṃ añ‍ñaṃ atthi, taṃ añ‍ñaṃ aikanikāyaṃpi nasamanupassāmī-ti yaujanā| ‘‘nikāya’’nti sattajātisamū haṃ|‘‘nissak‍kai karaṇavacana’’nti vibhattāpādānatthai karaṇavacanaṃ| aitaina tatau caraṇatau cittatauti atthaṃ vadati| gāthāyaṃ| ‘‘taṃ taṃ sabhāvau’’ti vijānanaphusanādikau sabhāvau, aggissauṇhauviya| ‘‘kic‍casampattiyaurasau’’ti taina taina dhammaina karaṇakic‍cañ‍ca, taṃ kic‍caṃ katvā laddhau sampattiguṇau ca| aggissa vatthumhi paripācanakic‍caṃ viya, aubhāsanaguṇauviya ca| ‘‘gayhākārau’’ti ñāṇaina gahaitabbau tassa tassa dhammassa dhajabhūtau ākārau| sampatti rasauyaiva vuc‍cati| ‘‘phalaṃvāpī’’ti kāriyapphalaṃ vāpi, aggissa dhūmauviya| ‘‘āsan‍nakāraṇa’’nti attanau anantarai phalanibbattakaṃ kāraṇaṃ, aggissa aggikāraka purisau viya| ‘‘ala’’nti samatthā| ‘‘vibuddhinau’’ti visaisabuddhi sampan‍nassa paṇḍitassa| ‘‘pubbaṅgamarasa’’nti ārammaṇaggahaṇai padhānarasa kic‍caṃ| ‘‘sandhāna pac‍cupaṭṭhāna’’nti nirantarappavattākārapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘nāma rūpapadaṭṭhāna’’nti phassādināmañ‍ca vatthu rūpañ‍cacittassapadaṭṭhānaṃ|

cittavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

14. ‘‘caitasi bhava’’nti cittasmiṃ pātubhūtaṃ| ‘‘aitaina siddhā hauntī’’ti sambandhau| ‘‘sā aiva phassādīnaṃ jāti| yācittassajarā, sā aiva phassādīnaṃjarā’’tiādinā yaujaitabbaṃ| ‘‘aikavaṇṭūpani bandhānī’’ti aikaina vaṇṭadaṇḍakaina upanibandhāni| ‘‘aikajātiyādi upanibandhā’’ti aikajātikathā divasaina upanibandhā| ‘‘cai’’ti caivadaiyya| ‘‘nā’’ti na vattabbaṃ| gāthāyaṃ| ‘‘dhammā’’ti nāmakkhandha dhammā| ‘‘manasā aivā’’ti kattubhūtaina manaina aiva| ‘‘pakatā’’ti pavattitā| ‘‘nimmitā’’ti nipphāditā| ‘‘cittakiriyā bhūtā aivā’’ti ārammaṇaṃ vijānantaṃ cittaṃ phusanākāraṃ janaitvāva vijānāti| sau phusanākārau phassauti vuc‍cati| avasaisā pana sabbaipi caitasikadhammā phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍janti| phassau haitu phassau pac‍cayau vaidanākkhandhassa upādāya| sañ‍ñākkhandhassa| saṅkhārakkhandhassa upādāyāti hi vuttaṃ| aivaṃ santaipi cittamūlakattā cittanissitattā ca taipidhammā cittakiri yābhūtā  aiva hauntīti| ‘‘aitainā’’ti aitainagāthāpadaina| vibhāvaniyaṃ pana aikālambaṇatā mattaina vibhāvaiti| paripuṇṇāni cai tasikaṅgāni upari thairaina sayamaiva vakkhamānattāti adhippāyau| idha pana padatthavibhāvanaṭṭhānattā paripuṇṇaihi aṅgaihi vibhāvaituṃ vaṭṭatīti āha ‘‘taṃ nasundara’’nti| ‘‘vatthumhī’’ti paṭakauṭṭhakādimhi| ‘‘nānācittakammānī’’ti hatthi assarūpādīni| vijānanamattaṃ cittaṃ, kusalanti vā akusalanti vā vattabbaṃ natthi| nānācaitasikai hi yuttattā aiva tathā vattabbaṃ hauti| vuttaṃhaitaṃ bhagavatā| pabhassaramidaṃ bhikkhavai cittaṃ| tañ‍ca khau āgantukaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhanti| taināha ‘‘udakaṃ viyacitta’’ntiādiṃ|

caitasikavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

15. ruppatīti padaṃ kattarivāhaitukammanivāsiddhaṃ| ruppanañ‍cavikārāpatti aivāti dassaituṃ ‘‘sītuṇhādīhī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ‘‘visamappavattivasainā’’ti dhātūnaṃ visamappavattivasaina| dhātukkhau bhavasaināti vuttaṃ hauti, ‘‘kainaruppatī’’ti aittha ‘‘kainā’’ti haitu atthaivā haitu kattarivā karaṇavacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| tathā sītainātiādīsupi| ḍaṃsamakasā nāma sūcimukhā khuddakamakkhikācaiva mahanta makkhikā ca| vātā nāma puratthimavātādayau| ātapau nāma sūriyātapau| sariṃsapānāma ahi vicchika satapadikādayau| taisaṃ samphassaihipi ruppati| maraṇaṃ vā gacchati, maraṇa mattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| ‘‘yai dhammā’’ti dvādasavidhā sappaṭigharūpadhammā| ‘‘añ‍ñaisa’’nti sauḷasan‍naṃ appaṭigharūpānañ‍ca arūpadhammānañ‍ca| ‘‘taisū’’ti niddhāraṇaibhummaṃ| idāni pāḷiyā saddhiṃ mukhyaruppanaṃ saṃsandaintau ‘‘samāgamau cā’’tiādimāha| samāgamau ca nāma añ‍ñamañ‍ñābhighaṭṭanaṃ vuc‍catīti sambandhau| āpātāgamanañ‍ca ārammaṇakaraṇañ‍ca ṭhapaitvāti yaujanā| ‘‘mahābhūtānamaiva vā’’ti āpaudhātu vaj‍jitānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ mahābhūtānamaiva vā| ‘‘vikāraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti vatvā tamaivatthaṃ vivarantau ‘‘yasmiṃ khaṇai’’tiādimāha| ‘‘sayaṃpi vikārapattā hauntī’’ti  taimahābhūtāsayaṃpi pakatiṃ vijahitvā aumattādhimattabhāvaṃ pāpuṇantīti atthau| ‘‘aumattādhimattarūpasantatīnañ‍cā’’ti paramparatau uppaj‍jamānā rūpasantatiyau sandhāya vuttaṃ| aivaṃ pāḷinayaina vipattivasaina ruppanaṃ vatvā idāni vipatti vā hautu, sampatti vā| purimapacchi masantatīnaṃ visadisappavattibhūtau vikāraupi aikaina pariyāyaina ruppanaṃnāmāti katvā puna taṃ ruppanaṃ dassaintau ‘‘apicaitthā’’tiādimāha| ‘‘ghaṭṭanavasaina ruppana dhammāna maivā’’ti āpātāgamanādivasaina ruppanadhammaihivinā abhighaṭṭanavasainaruppana sabhāvānaṃ rūpadhammānamaiva siddhanti pucchā| ‘‘saviggahā hauntī’’ti dabba saṇṭhānākārasahitā haunti| kasmā, auḷārikasabhāvattā| bahūnañ‍carūpakalāpānaṃ aikakkhaṇai aikābaddhabhāvainapavattattā| arūpadhammāhi saṇhasukhumasabhāvā ca haunti| sacai anaikasatasahassānipi aikatau pavattaiyyuṃ| dabbasaṇṭhānabhāvaṃ nagamissantiyaiva| aikasmiñ‍ca sattasantānai aikakkhaṇai aika kalāpauva pavattati| kasmā, anantara pac‍cayūpanibandhaina pavattattā| tasmātai saviggahāna haunti| rūpadhammā pana auḷārika sabhāvā ca haunti| bahūnaṃ san‍nicayaisati dabbasaṇṭhānatthāya saṃvattanti| aikakkhaṇai ca bahukalāpāpi aikābaddhāhutvā pavattanti| tasmā taisaviggahāhauntīti|

‘‘sītādiggahaṇasāmatthiyainā’’ti sītainapi ruppati, uṇhainapi ruppatītiādinā laukassa pac‍cakkhatau pākaṭassa sītādivacanassasāmatthiyaina| tañhi vacanaṃ laukassa apākaṭaṃ arūpadhammānaṃ ruppanaṃ idhanādhippaitanti dīpaitīti adhippāyau| ‘‘vauhārau nāmā’’ti nāmasañ‍ñā nāmāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘laukaupacārainā’’ti bahujanassa upacāraina vauhāraina kathanaina| ‘‘pākaṭa nimittavasainaivā’’ti pākaṭassa saddappavattinimittassa vasainaiva| ‘‘sītādiggahaṇaina vināpī’’ti pisaddaina sītādiggahaṇasāmatthiyainapīti dīpaiti| aivaṃ santaipi pāḷisādhakaṃ nāma na sak‍kāsabbattha laddhuṃ| pākaṭanimitta vacanamaiva sabbattha sādhāraṇanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘tappasaṅganivattī’’ti tassa arūpadhammānaṃ rūpatāpasaṅgassa nivatti| ‘‘iddhivikubbanāvasappavattā’’ti aittha iddhivikubbanānāmaiddhiyānānappakāramāpanaṃ| ‘‘rūpatā siddhī’’ti  rūpanti nāma sañ‍ñāsiddhi| ‘‘idaṃ panā’’ti idaṃ rūpaṃ pana| ‘‘anuggahānaṃ sītādīnaṃ vasainā’’ti kiñ‍cāpipāḷiyaṃ sītādivacanaṃ upaghātakānaṃ sītādīnaṃ vasaina vuttaṃ| taica brahmalaukai natthi| anuggāhakā aiva atthi| taisaṃvasaināti adhippāyau| ‘‘pāḷiyaṃ niddiṭṭhānī’’ti sañ‍jānātīti khau bhikkhavai tasmā sañ‍ñāti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca sañ‍jānāti| nīlaṃpi sañ‍jānāti, pītampi sañ‍jānātī-tiādinā ca, vijānātīti khau bhikkhavai tasmā viñ‍ñāṇanti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca vijānāti| madhuraṃpi vijānāti, ambilaṃpi vijānātī-tiādinā ca-pāḷiyaṃ niddiṭṭhāni|

rūpapadavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|
