pubbainivāsañ‍ñāṇaina atītai bhavai ahaṃ dvipādakau ahausintivā catuppādakau ahausinti vā tatau cutau asukabhavai uppan‍nauti vā na jānāti na passatīti| vuc‍catai| sā pana kaṅkhā dhamma mattappavatti dassanatau paṭṭhāya antarāyika kaṅkhā na hauti| vicikicchā paṭirūpikā aiva| sā hi ariyānaṃpi labbhati yaivāti| tathā anāgatai phalapañ‍cakaṃ passantau aparantai pañ‍cavidhaṃ kaṅkhaṃ atik‍kamati| pac‍cuppan‍nai phalapañ‍cakañ‍ca haitupañ‍cakañ‍ca passantau pac‍cuppan‍nai kaṅkhā chak‍kaṃ atik‍kamati| saisaṃ sabbaṃ pubbantai vuttanayamaivāti| aṭṭhavidhāya kaṅkhāya atik‍kamanai pana paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumappaṭilaumaṃ passantau daisanā dhammai ca laukuttara dhammaisu ca kaṅkhaṃ atik‍kamati| dhammai kaṅkhātik‍kamanaina sahaiva daisaintai satthari ca, yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipan‍nai saṅghai ca, tīsu sikkhāsu ca, yathāvuttai pubbantai ca, aparantai ca, pubbantā parantaisu ca, paṭic‍casamuppādaina saha paṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu ca sabbaṃ kaṅkhaṃ atik‍kamatiyaiva| taināha ‘‘sabhāva  dhammappavattiyā’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘sabhāvadhammappavattiyā cā’’ti pac‍cayaparamparāvasaina sabhāvadhammānaṃ paramparappavattiyā ca| dhammasu dhammatā nāma daisanā dhammassa svākkhātatādibhāvaina sundara dhammatā| anavaj‍jadhammatā| buddhasubuddhatā nāma taṃ dhammaṃ daisaintassa buddhassa sabbañ‍ñubuddhatā|

pariyuṭṭhitānaṃ diṭṭhivicikicchānaṃ vikkhambhanaṃ nāma haiṭṭhā dhammavavatthāna vasaina pac‍cayapariggahavasaina ca sijjhati| vipassanā kammaṃ pana anusayabhūtānaṃ diṭṭhivicikicchānaṃ sal‍likhanatthāya karīyatīti vuttaṃ ‘‘anusaya samūhananatthañ‍cā’’tiādi| ‘‘tattha panā’’ti tasmiṃ paṭisambhidā maggai pana| vibhattaisūti pāṭhasaisau| ‘‘yassā’’ti yaugī puggalassa| ‘‘yaṃ dūrai santikaivā’’ti yaṃ dūrai pavattaṃ rūpaṃ vā, santikai pavattaṃ rūpaṃvāti pāṭhasaisau| ‘‘tadatthassa panā’’ti sammasitadhammuddhāratthassa| ‘‘ñāṇaina daḷhaṃ gahaitvā’’ti dhammavavatthānañ‍ñāṇaina suṭṭhuvibhūtaṃ katvāti adhippāyau| idāni yaṃ yaṃ daḷhaṃ gahaitabbaṃ| taṃ taṃ sarūpatau dassaituṃ ‘‘tatthā’’tiādimāha| puna daḷhaṃ gahitassa rūpassa anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘sā ca kriyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ‘‘nassantī’’ti bhij‍janti| anic‍cataṃ gacchanti| ‘‘vitthambhana kriyā’’ti aittha vividhaina thambhanaṃ vitthambhanaṃ| nānādisābhimukhavasaina thambhanaṃ vahananti atthau| ‘‘imāsañ‍cā’’ti catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānañ‍ca| visuṃ visunti vuttattā tāsaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñañ‍ca sahajātaisu gahaṇaṃ sambhavatīti vuttaṃ ‘‘āpādīnaṃ’’ti| āpādīnaṃ saisamahābhūtānañ‍cāti atthau| santatiyā sarīrāni santati sarīrāni| ‘‘tathā tathā kappaitvā’’ti rūpadhammānaṃ santati parivatti nāma abhik‍kamantassa aivaṃ bhavaiyya, paṭik‍kamantassa aivaṃ bhavaiyya, tathā samiñ‍jantassa pasāraintassātiādinā parikappaitvā| ‘‘saisakhandhaisupi taṃ taṃ santati nānattaṃ’’ti vaidanākkhandhai tāva kāyai kāyikasukhasantati dukkhasantatīnaṃ nānattaṃ| cittai saumanassa daumanassa santatīnaṃ| upaikkhā santati pana avibhūtā hauti| kappaitvā gahaitabbā| yadā cittai saumanassa daumanassāni na sandissanti, tadā idāni upaikkhā mai vaidanā pavattatīti vā, yadā tāni sandissanti, tadā sā nirujjhatīti vā aivaṃ tak‍kaitvā sammasitabbāti vuttaṃ hauti| sañ‍ñākkhandhai rūpasañ‍ñāsaddasañ‍ñādīnaṃ vasaina| saṅkhārakkhandhai rūpasañ‍caitanā saddasañ‍caitanādīnaṃ vasaina, nānāvitak‍ka vicārādīnaṃ vasaina, laubhālaubha dausādausa mauhāmauhādīnaṃ vasaina ca| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhai cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ, kusalā kusalaviñ‍ñāṇādīnañ‍ca vasaina santati nānattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| vissāsaṃ karautīti vatvā tadatthaṃ vivarati ‘‘aitaṃ mai’’tiādinā| tattha tayauvissāsā taṇhāvissāsau, mānavissāsau, diṭṭhivissāsau| tattha aitaṃ mamāti taṇhāvissāsau| aisauhamasmīti mānavissāsau| aisau mai attāti diṭṭhivissāsau| ‘‘pariharatī’’ti bhavābhavaisu pariggahaitvā harati vahati| ‘‘paramajjhattabhāvainā’’ti padhānaajjhattabhāvaina| ajjhattasārabhāvaināti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘pariggahitau’’ti aisau mai attāti diṭṭhipariggahaina pariggahitau| tayau hi pariggahā taṇhā pariggahau, mānapariggahau, diṭṭhipariggahauti| vasai sabbasau vattantau nāma kaivalaṃ vasāyattavuttikau aiva siyā, na pac‍cayāyattavuttikauti dassaintau ‘‘yathāvuttaihī’’tiādimāha| tattha ‘‘yathāvuttaihī’’ti sabbasaṅgahai vuttappakāraihi kammābhisaṅkharaṇādīhi| ‘‘tathāpariggahaitabbau’’ti aisau mai attāti aivaṃ paramajjhattabhāvaina pariggahaitabbau| tathā hi yau attanau vasaina vattati, tasmiṃ attanau sāmibhāvau natthi| yasmiñ‍ca sāmibhāvau natthi, tasmiṃ rūpādikai dhammai attānaṃ sāmikaṃ katvā aisau mama attāti pariggahau nāma micchā aiva| tathā yau ca khaṇika dhammau hauti, khaṇai khaṇai bhij‍jati, taṃ rūpādikaṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ attanau sāraṃ katvā tathā pariggahaupi micchā aiva| aittha ca ‘‘micchā aivā’’ti paramattha sac‍caṃ patvā micchā aiva| vipal‍lāsau aivāti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘sukhaṃ viddhaṃsaitī’’ti kāyikasukhañ‍ca caitasika sukhañ‍ca viddhaṃsaiti| vināsaiti| ‘‘nānādukkhaṃ janaitī’’ti kāyikadukkhañ‍ca caitasika dukkhañ‍ca janaiti uppādaiti| ‘‘idañ‍ca rūpaṃ aidisamaiva hautī’’ti kathaṃ hauti| manussattabhāvai ṭhitānaṃ sattānaṃ yathāpavattaṃ  manussa sukhaṃ viddhaṃsaiti, jiṇṇabhin‍nabhāvaṃ pāpaiti, nānādukkhaṃ janaiti| sayaṃ apāyattabhāvaṃ gahaitvā taisaṃ nānāapāya dukkhaṃ janaiti| tathā daivattabhāvai sak‍kattabhāvai brahmattabhāvai ṭhitānantiādinā vattabbaṃ| tathā manussattabhāvai ṭhitānaṃ nānāvipariṇāma kic‍caihi yathāpavattaṃ sukhaṃ viddhaṃsaiti, nānāpac‍cayābhisaṅkharaṇakic‍caihi nānādukkhaṃ janaiti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘idañ‍ca rūpaṃ aidisamaivā’’ti| ‘‘sāraunāma thāmabalavisaisau vuc‍catī’’ti aitaina thāmabalavisaisaina sampan‍naṃ vatthuṃpi saṅgaṇhāti| ‘‘asārakaṭṭhainā’’ti aittha sārau aiva sārakaṃ, na sārakaṃ asārakanti imamatthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘rūpaṃ pana sayaṃpi aivarūpau sārau na hautī’’ti vuttaṃ| sattānaṃ attasārau jīvasārau na hautīti atthau| natthi sārau aitassāti asārakanti imamatthaṃ sandhāya ‘‘na ca aivarūpaina sāraina yuttaṃ’’ti vuttaṃ| attasāraina jīvasāraina saṃyuttaṃ hautīti atthau| tattha purimau atthau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassatīti idaṃ sandhāya vuttau| pacchimau rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃti imāni sandhāya vuttau| aitaina asārakaṭṭhaināti asārabhāvaina, asārayuttabhāvaina vāti ca| anattāti aitthapi na attā anattā, natthi attā aitassāti vā anattāti ca dvidhā atthau sijjhati| ‘‘yañ‍cā’’ti yañ‍ca rūpaṃ| ‘‘pīḷaitī’’ti taṃ samaṅgī puggalaṃ pīḷaiti| aitaina anattabhāvai siddhai dukkhabhāvaupi siddhauti dassaiti| ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ vayānaṃ vasaina addhābhaidau yaujaitabbau’’ti pathamavayai pavattaṃ rūpaṃ dutīyavayaṃ na pāpuṇāti, pathamavayai aiva nirujjhatītiādinā addhāpac‍cuppan‍nai antaugadhabhaidau yaujaitabbau| ‘‘sabhāgaikasantānavasainā’’ti sabhāgānaṃ dhammānaṃ sabhāgaṭṭhaina aikībhūtā santati sabhāgaikasantati| ‘‘sabhāgānaṃ dhammānaṃ’’ti ca cittajaisutāva nirantarappavattaina laubhasampayuttacittaina sahajātā rūpadhammā sabhāgarūpadhammā nāma| tathā nāma dhammā ca| aivaṃ dausasampayuttādīsupi| utujaisu nirantarappavattaina sītautunā samuṭṭhitā rūpadhammā sabhāgarūpa dhammā nāma| tathā uṇhautunā samuṭṭhitā| aisanayau āhārajaisupīti  aivaṃ sabhāgaikasantativasaina| ‘‘sampāpuṇituṃ’’ti ñāṇaina sampāpuṇituṃ| ‘‘addhāsantativasainā’’ti aittha santativasaina sammasanaṃ visaisatau adhippaitaṃ| aivañ‍ca katvā haiṭṭhā anic‍caṃ khayaṭṭhaināti padai catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ santati vibhāganayau suṭṭhu dassitauti| ‘‘anic‍calakkhaṇa dassanamaiva padhānaṃ’’ti imasmiṃ ñāṇaipi udayabbayañ‍ñāṇaipi bhaṅgañ‍ñāṇaipi anic‍calakkhaṇa dassanamaiva padhānaṃ| bhayañ‍ñāṇai ca ādīnavañ‍ñāṇai ca nibbidāñāṇai ca dukkhalakkhaṇa dassanaṃ padhānaṃ| upari catūsu ñāṇaisu anattalakkhaṇa dassanaṃ padhānanti| ‘‘samuditaisū’’ti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ avinābhāvasahāyabhāvaina samuppan‍naisu| idañ‍ca avij‍jāsamudayā rūpasamudayau| taṇhāsamudayā upādāna samudayau| āhāra samudayā rūpasamudayauti pāḷivasaina vuttaṃ| appahīna vasaināti pāḷipadai pana avij‍jā taṇhā diṭṭhānusayānaṃ appahīnaṭṭhaina santānai vij‍jamānatā aiva pamāṇanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘samudaintī’’ti paṭisandhitau paṭṭhāya aikatau udainti| uparūpari ainti| āgacchantīti atthau| aikabhavabhāvaina pātubbhavantīti adhippāyau| taināha ‘‘yāvamaraṇakālā’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘pasavantī’’ti pavaḍḍhamānā savanti, pavattanti, sandanti| ‘‘taisu panā’’ti avij‍jā taṇhupādāna kammaisu| ‘‘nirujjhantī’’ti tasmiṃbhavai aiva nirujjhanti| tasmiṃ bhavai niraudhau nāma bhavantarai puna anuppādau yaivāti vuttaṃ ‘‘bhavantarai’’tiādi| ‘‘taṃ santatiyaṃ’’ti tasmiṃ pac‍cuppan‍na bhavaiti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘puna anuppādasaṅkhātaṃ niraudhaṃ’’ti santati niraudhau nāma vuttau| na nibbāna niraudhau| aitāhi pac‍cayai sati, bhavantarai uppaj‍jīssanti yaivāti| idāni yathāpavatta santatīsu pariyāpan‍nānaṃ sabbapacchimānaṃ nāmarūpānaṃ khaṇikaniraudhavasaina atthaṃ vadantau ‘‘yathāpavatta santatiyau vā chij‍janti bhij‍jantī’’ti vuttaṃ| ‘‘udayabbayaṃ samanupassantassa cā’’ti aittha katamau udayau nāma, katamau vayau nāma, kathañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ samanupassatīti āha ‘‘udayabbayaṃ passantassāti aittha yathā nāmā’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘tamau khandhau’’ti tamaurāsi| ‘‘vaitī’’ti vigacchati| antara dhāyati|‘‘udayau’’ti uppādau caiva vaḍḍhi ca| ‘‘vayau’’ti vigamanaṃ, antaradhānaṃ| ‘‘nānāpayaugavasainavā’’ti samiñ‍janappasāraṇādi nānāpayaugavasaina vā| udayapakkhānaṃ nānāsantatīnaṃ udayañ‍ca udayatau| vayapakkhānaṃ nānāsantatīnaṃ vayañ‍ca vayatauti yaujanā|

baujjhaṅga dhammaihi sampan‍nattā ārādhaiti attanau cittaṃti āraddhau| tuṭṭhacittauti atthau| vipassatīti vipassakau| āraddhau hutvā vipassakau āraddhavipassakau| duvidhauhi vipassakau taruṇa vipassakau ca āraddha vipassakau ca| tattha khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ suṭṭhu apassantau dubbalavipassakau taruṇa vipassakau nāma| khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ suṭṭhu passantau vipassanā kammai ārādhita cittau balavavipassakau āraddhavipassakau nāma| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ|

[ka]

‘suñ‍ñāgārai paviṭṭhassa, santacittassa bhikkhunau|

amānusī ratī hauti, samma dhammaṃ vipassatau,ti ca||

[kha]

‘yau ca vassasataṃ jīvai, apassaṃ udayabbayaṃ|

aikāhaṃ jīvitaṃ saiyyau, passatau udayabbayaṃ,ti ca||

ayamidha adhippaitau| vipassanaṃ upaic‍ca kilaisainti, malīnabhāvaṃ gamaintīti vipassanupak‍kilaisā| paribandhanti nīvāraintīti paribandhakāti haiṭṭhā vuttamaiva antarāyika dhammānaṃ nāmaṃ| ‘‘sarīraubhāsau’’ti sakalasarīratau nikkhantau aubhāsau| ‘‘pañ‍cavidhā pītī’’ti khuddikā pīti, khaṇikā pīti, auk‍kantikā pīti, ubbaigā pīti, pharaṇā pītīti| ‘‘duvidhā passaddhī’’ti atibalavantī kāyapassaddhicaiva cittapassaddhi ca| vipassanā kammai aukappanākārappavattā saddhāaiva adhimuc‍canaṭṭhaina adhimaukkhau saddhādhimaukkhau| ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’ti tasmiṃ kammai anikkhittadhuraṃ paggahitasīsaṃ vīriyaṃ| ‘‘sukhaṃ’’ti cittasukhaṃ| taināha ‘‘saumanassaṃ’’ti| ‘‘vipassanāñāṇaṃ’’ti indavissaṭṭhavajirasadisaṃ balavavipassanāñāṇaṃ| ‘‘satī’’ti tasmiṃ kammai appamuṭṭhā sati| ‘‘tatramajjhattupaikkhā’’ti cittassa  līnuddhac‍cādīhi visamantaihi vimuttā tatramajjhattatāsaṅkhātā upaikkhā| ‘‘āvaj‍janupaikkhā’’ti vasibhāvapattā āvaj‍janasaṅkhātā upaikkhā| ‘‘ālayaṃ’’ti taṇhālayaṃ| ‘‘kurumānā’’ti karauntī| ‘‘sukhumā taṇhā’’ti kusalaihi missakattā idaṃ akusalantipi jānituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyā sukhumataṇhā| ‘‘na vatamai’’ti mama na uppan‍napubbauvatāti yaujanā| ‘‘assādasahitā’’ti aubhāsādīsu assādataṇhā sahitā| ‘‘adhimāna vasainā’’ti adhikataraṃ mañ‍ñanāvasaina| ‘‘tāvadaivā’’ti nikantiyā uppattikkhaṇaiyaiva| ‘‘nikāmanākārau’’ti icchanākārau| assādanākārau| ‘‘nikāmanassavatthū’’ti nikantiyā vatthu| ‘‘addhā’’ti aikantaina| ‘‘handā’’ti cittavissaṭṭhakaraṇai nipātapadaṃ| idānaiva naṃ visaudhaimi, na ciraṃ pavattituṃ dassāmīti imamatthaṃ dīpaiti| ‘‘taisvaivā’’ti taisuaubhāsādīsu aiva| aṭṭhakathāya na samaiti| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ nikanti vikkhambhanassa visuṃ vuttattāti adhippāyau| vipassanāparipākau nāma visaisantarappavattaina pākaṭauti dassaituṃ ‘‘saṅkhārupaikkhābhāvaṃ pattā’’tiādimāha| vuṭṭhānagāmini vipassanābhāvapattaināti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘dvai vipassanā cittānī’’ti upacārānulaumacittāni| sau hi vuṭṭhānanti vuc‍catīti sambandhau| saṅkhāra nimittatau vā vuṭṭhāti| nibbānaṃ ārabbha pavattitau| yadi aivaṃ gautrabhupi vuṭṭhānaṃ nāma siyāti| na siyā| maggassa āvaj‍janamattai ṭhitattā| paṭivaidhakic‍cassaca sammauha samucchaidakic‍cassa abhāvatau| ‘‘sayaṃ’’ti maggasaṅkhātau sayaṃ| ‘‘taṃ samaṅgīpuggalaṃ vā’’ti taina maggaina samaṅgīpuggalaṃ vā| ‘‘vaṭṭappavattatau cā’’ti āyatiṃ bhavaisu apāyavaṭṭappavattatau vā, aṭṭhama bhavatau paṭṭhāya kāmasugati vaṭṭappavattatau vā| avassaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ gamaiti sampāpaitīti vuṭṭhānagāminīti vacanatthau| ‘‘avassaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ gacchantī’’ti idha ṭhitānaṃ vuṭṭhānatau nivatti nāma natthīti adhippāyau| idañ‍ca pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ buddhasāvakānañ‍ca vasaina daṭṭhabbaṃ| sabbañ‍ñu baudhisattā pana pubbabuddhānaṃ sāsanai pabbajitvā vipassanā kammaṃ ārabhantā anulaumañ‍ñāṇaṃ āhac‍ca ṭhapaintīti aṭṭhaka thāsu  vuttaṃ| tattha ‘‘āhac‍cā’’ti idaṃ saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇassa matthakapattidassanatthaṃ vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘āvaj‍janaṭṭhāniyaṃ’’ti tatau pubbabhāgai kainaci cittaina aggahitai nibbānā rammaṇai āvaj‍janaṭṭhāniyaṃ| ‘‘taṃ’’ti gautrabhuñ‍ñāṇaṃ| gauttaṃ bhavati abhibhavati chindatīti gautrabhu| gauttaṃ vā abhisambhuṇāti pāpuṇātīti gautrabhūti vacanatthau| kathaṃ pana gautrabhu cittaṃ gautrabhuñ‍ñāṇaṃ puthuj‍janagauttaṃ chindati, ariyagauttaṃ pāpuṇātīti yuj‍jaiyya, sauhi puggalau tasmiṃkhaṇai puthuj‍janagauttapariyāpan‍nauaivāti| sac‍caṃ| attani pana chin‍nattā sayaṃ chindati nāma| nibbānassa ca ariyagaucarattā taṃ ārammaṇa karaṇavasaina ariyagauttaṃ pāpuṇāti nāmāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| pac‍cayasattivisaisaina attanau anantarai ariyagauttaṃ bhāvaiti, pātubhāvaitīti gautrabhūtipi yuj‍jati| ‘‘paricchij‍jā’’ti paricchinditvā| diṭṭhivicikicchādīhi sabbavipphandanaihi muñ‍citvāti vuttaṃ hauti| sammauhassa pahānamaiva daṭṭhabbaṃ| na tai dhammai ārammaṇa karaṇa vasaina jānanaṃ| kathaṃ tai dhammai ārammaṇaṃ akarauntau maggau tai dhammai parijānāti nāmāti| kic‍casiddhi vasaina| sau hi sammauhaṃ pajahantau ārammaṇaṃ karauntānipi hi pubbabhāgañ‍ñāṇāni tappahānakic‍cassa asādhakattā dukkha sac‍caṃ parijānantānīti na vuc‍canti| maggau pana ārammaṇaṃ akarauntaupi tappahānakic‍cassa sādhakattātaṃ sac‍caṃ parijānātīti vuc‍cati| kasmā| tatau paṭṭhāya tassapuggalassa sabbakālaṃ tasmiṃsac‍cai parijānana kic‍cassa siddhattā| yathāhi aikau purisau cakkhumhi pittasaimhādidausaihi palibuddhattā andhau hauti| na kiñ‍ci passati| yadā pana sau aikaṃ taijavantaṃ añ‍janaṃ labhitvā cakkhuṃ añ‍jayantau sabbarattiṃ niddāyati| tassa niddāyantassaiva cakkhumhi sabbadausā nassanti| cakkhu vippasan‍naṃ hauti| taṅkhaṇatau paṭṭhāya sau niddāyantaupi rūpāni passatīti vuc‍cati| kasmā, andhabhāvavigamaina dassanakic‍cassa siddhattā| aivamaivamidaṃ daṭṭhabbanti| aikadaisaina vā haiṭṭhimamaggaisu, anavasaisaina vā arahattamaggai| ‘‘apacchāvattikaṃ’’ti aittha pacchatau āvattanaṃ pacchāvattaṃ| natthi pacchāvattaṃ assāti apacchāvattikanti  viggahau| ‘‘puna anuppādadhammatāpattivasaina niraudhasaṅkhātaṃ’’ti maggakkhaṇai aiva tānivaṭṭāni puna anuppādasabhāvaṃ āpaj‍janti, nirujjhanti| tathā niraudhasaṅkhātaṃ| ‘‘uppādanasaṅkhātāya bhāvanāyā’’ti aitaina maggassa aikacittakkhaṇikattā vaḍḍhanasaṅkhātāya bhāvanāya aukāsau natthīti katvā vuttaṃ| ariyamaggaṃ paṭilābhatthāya pana pavattitā pubbabhāgabhāvanāpi maggabhāvanā aiva| sā ca ariyamaggaṃ patvā matthakapattā hauti| tasmā ariyamaggau aiva matthakapattau bhāvanānuttarau hautīti imināpariyāyaina vaḍḍhana saṅkhātāya bhāvanāyātipi yuj‍jatiyaiva| attanau anantarai pavattaṃ ānantarikaṃ| ānantarikaṃ phalaṃ aitassāti viggahau| tassa ca tatauparaṃ dvai vā tīṇi vā phalacittāni pavattantīti iminā sambandhau| apanītau aggi aitasmāti apanītaggikau| ‘‘nibbāpaintau viyā’’ti aitaina paṭippassambhanappahānakic‍caina maggānukulappavattiṃ dassaiti| ‘‘taisaṃ’’ti taisaṃ cakkhūnaṃ| taṃ rūpaṃ tassa pākaṭaṃ na tāva hautīti sambandhau| ‘‘tassā’’ti tassa puggalassa| ‘‘pac‍cavaikkhanavāraisū’’ti aittha pasādacakkhunā diṭṭhai pavattā anubandhaka vīthiyaupi saṅgayhanti|

uparimaggaihi vadhitabbā māraitabbāti uparimaggavajjhā| ‘‘kilaisavibhāgaisū’’ti asukamaggaina asukākilaisā pahīyantīti aivaṃ kilaisavibhāgaisu| ‘‘pāḷiyaṃ’’ti paṭisambhidā maggapāḷiyaṃ| vuc‍camānai pana vattabbanti sambandhau| maggaṅgabaujjhaṅgādīnaṃ pac‍cavaikkhanaṃ nāma maggapac‍cavaikkhanamaiva| tathā pacchā vā pavattatīti siddhaṃ| ‘‘aviruddhā hautī’’ti visuddhimaggaina asamaintāpi pac‍cavaikkhantānaṃ icchānurūpasambhavatauti adhippāyau| ‘‘adhimānaniddaisa aṭṭhakathāsū’’ti vinayai catutthapārājikai adhimānapadaniddaisa aṭṭhakathāsu| ‘‘sau’’ti ariyabhūtau bhikkhu| ‘‘nau laddhuṃ na vaṭṭatī’’ti aladdhuṃ na vaṭṭatīti atthau| laddhuṃ nau na vaṭṭatīti vā yaujanā| catubbidhaṃ maggañ‍ñāṇaṃ gahitaṃ| ñāṇadassanavisuddhi nāmāti vuttattā|

‘‘abhinivisanaṃ’’ti ajjhattaṃ daḷhataraṃ patiṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘nikhātasinairupā dassaviyā’’ti  tassa pavattanaṃ viya| ‘‘kaivalaṃ pac‍cayāyatta vuttitāya adiṭṭhattā’’ti aitaina aisau mai attā, na parassa attāti aivaṃ attānaṃ attassa sāmikaṃ katvā gahaṇakāraṇaṃ vadati| tattha kaivalasaddaina paramattha sac‍caṃ patvā attassaiva abhāvatau khandhapañ‍cakassa attanauvasāyatta vuttitaṃ nivattaiti| sammuti sac‍caṃ pana patvā attassa vij‍jamānattā mahājanau attanau khandhapañ‍cakaṃ attanau vasāyatta vuttiṃ mañ‍ñamānau aisau mai attāti gaṇhāti| ‘‘khaṇabhaṅgassa ca adiṭṭhattā’’ti aitaina aisau mai sāraṭṭhaina attā| aisau mama sārau| aitaṃ mama sarīraṃ| yāva vassasataṃpi vassasahassampi aitaṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ na bhij‍jati, na vinassati| tāva ahaṃ na marāmi| yadā aitaṃ bhij‍jati, vinassati| tadā ahaṃ marāmi| ahañ‍ca mama khandhapañ‍cakañ‍ca advayaṃ anānattanti aivaṃ gahaṇakāraṇaṃ vadati| tattha ‘‘khaṇabhaṅgassā’’ti nāmarūpadhammānaṃ khaṇai khaṇai bhañ‍janassa bhij‍janassa maraṇassa| idampanamaraṇaṃ sammuti sac‍cai natthi| paramattha sac‍cai aiva atthi| tasmā taṃ apassantau mahājanau attanau khandhapañ‍cakaṃ attanausāraṃ mañ‍ñamānau aisau mai attāti gaṇhātīti| ‘‘taṃ dvayaṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ katvā’’ti khandhapañ‍cakassa kaivalaṃ pac‍cayāyatta vuttitañ‍ca khaṇabhaṅgañ‍ca ñāṇa cakkhunā suṭṭhudiṭṭhaṃ katvā| tattha pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ suṭṭhupassantau kaivalaṃ pac‍cayāyatta vuttitañ‍ca avasāyatta vuttitañ‍ca suṭṭhu passati| yau ca attanau vasāyattavutti na hauti| sau paramatthasac‍caṃ patvā aisau mama santakantipi vattuṃ nārahati| kutau mama attāti| api ca, sattau nāma sabhāvatau natthi| pañ‍ñattimattaṃ hauti| yau ‘ahanti vā, maiti vā, mamāti vā, gayhaiyya| asantai ca attasmiṃ kau mama attā nāma bhavaiyyāti| khaṇabhaṅgau hauti| khaṇai khaṇai bhij‍jati| sattau ca nāma aikasmiṃbhavai ādimhi sakiṃ aiva jāyati| antai sakiṃ aiva marati| antarā vassasataṃpi vassasahassaṃpi maraṇaṃ nāma natthi| añ‍ñāhi khaṇikadhammānaṃ gati| añ‍ñā sattassa| aivaṃsantai kathaṃ khaṇikadhammā sattassa sāraṭṭhaina attānāma bhavaiyyunti| tasmā tai avasavattanaṭṭhaina asārakaṭṭhaina ca anattā aiva| apica  ‘‘avasavattanaṭṭhainā’’ti aittha vasau nāma sattasantānai aikau padhānadhammau| sacai attānāma atthi| sau aiva padhānabhūtassa vasassapi attā bhavaiyya| aivaṃsati, yau vasau, sau attā| yau attā, sau vasauti bhavaiyya| tathā ca sati, rūpaṃ attāti gaṇhantau rūpañ‍ca vasañ‍ca attānañ‍ca aikattaṃ katvā gaṇhāti nāma| tathā vaidanaṃ attāti| sañ‍ñaṃ, saṅkhārai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attāti gaṇhantaipi| aivañ‍casati, sattau ca khandhapañ‍cakañ‍ca vasau ca attā ca sabbamaitaṃ aikattaṃ gacchati, aikau sattauti saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| na hi imasmiṃ vasavattanaṭṭhai aikassasattassa dvai attā tayau attā bahūattāti sak‍kā bhavituṃ| atha yathā yathā sattassa vasau vattati, icchā pavattati| tathā tathā khandhapañ‍cakampi nic‍cakālaṃ sattassavasaṃ anugacchaiyya| kasmā, sattassa ca khandhapañ‍cakassa ca vasassa ca nānattābhāvatau| aivaṃ anugacchantai sati| rūpaṃ mai attā, vaidanā mai attātiādiggahaṇaṃ vaṭṭaiyya| sabhāvatau aviruddhaṃ bhavaiyya| na pana tathā anugacchati| na hi dubbaṇṇau durūpau purisau attanau rūpakāyaṃ attanau vasainaiva suvaṇṇaṃ surūpaṃ kātuṃ sak‍kauti| tathā dussaddauvā susaddaṃ, duggandhauvā sugandhaṃ| na ca andhauvā anandhaṃ| badhirauvā abadhiraṃ| raugīvā araugaṃ| kuṭṭhīvā akuṭṭhaṃ| gaṇḍīvā agaṇḍaṃ| jiṇṇauvā taruṇaṃ| ajaraṃ vā amaraṇaṃ vā kātuṃ sak‍kauti| nāpi apāyaisu ca apatantaṃ| patitvā vā tatau vimuttaṃ kātuṃ sak‍kauti| tathā daivau vā daivattā acāvaituṃ| sak‍kauvā sak‍kattā, mārau vā mārattā, brahmā vā brahmattāti| aisanayau saisakhandhaisupi| tathā pana avasānugamanatau paramattha sac‍caṃ patvā khandhapañ‍cakai attanau sāmikic‍caṃ issarakic‍caṃ nāma natthi| asāmikamaiva taṃ hauti, anissaraṃ| tathā ca sati, attānaṃ tassa sāmiṃ issaraṃ katvā rūpaṃ mai attāti gahaṇaṃ micchāgahaṇaṃ nāma hautīti| laukai pana mahājanau dhammānaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ na jānanti, na passanti, na paṭivijjhanti| taisaṃ parampara pac‍cayavasaina pavattamānai khandhasantānai sammuti sac‍cavasaina nānāvasavattanākārā nāma santi| nānāsāmi issarādhipativauhārā ca laukai sandissanti| tai  sammuti sac‍cavasaina uppan‍naisu taisu sāmiissarādhipati vauhāraisu vauhāramattai aṭhatvā sabhāvasac‍caṃ mañ‍ñantā micchāgahaṇaṃ gaṇhanti| aittha siyā| na nu vasaunāma icchā| sattau ca gantuṃ icchantau gacchati| ṭhātuṃ icchantau tiṭṭhati| aivaṃsati| tassa khandhapañ‍cakaṃ tassa vasāyatta vutti aiva hautīti| na hauti| kasmā, pac‍cayāyatta vuttittā aiva| tathāhi gantuṃ icchānāma bahiddhā dhanahaituvā uppaj‍jaiyya| nānābhaugahaitu vā| añ‍ñaina vā nānākāraṇaina| tattha yaisaṃ dhanai asati, yaṃ yaṃ dukkhaṃ āgacchaiyya kāyikaṃ vā caitasikaṃ vā| taisaṃ taṃ taṃ dukkhapac‍cayā dhanicchā nāma uppaj‍jati| dhanicchā pac‍cayā ganticchādayau uppaj‍janti| tathā nānābhaugicchā nānākammicchāsu cāti| nānādukkhabhayupaddavamūlikāsu icchāsu vattabba maiva natthi| tathā nānārammaṇānaṃ palaubhana dukkhamūlikāsu ca nānāvitak‍kānaṃ vipphandanamūlikāsu ca icchāsu| aivaṃ vasaupi pac‍cayāyatta vuttikau aiva hauti| ‘‘gamanaṃ’’ti gamanākāraina pavattaṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ| yainayainadhanādihaitunā sau sau vasau uppaj‍jati| gamanampi taina tainaiva uppaj‍jati āhārupatthambhanādipac‍cayaina ca| vasau pana gamanassa pac‍cayaika daisamattaṃ hauti| sau hi tadañ‍ñapac‍cayaihi vinā sayaṃ uppaj‍jituṃ na sak‍kauti| kutau gamanaṃ uppādaituṃ| aivaṃ ṭhānādīsu| aivañ‍ca sati, idaṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ pac‍cayāyatta vuttikantic‍caiva vattuṃ arahati| nau vasāyatta vuttikanti| api ca gantuṃ icchantau gacchatīti pañ‍ñatti mattabhūtau sattau gacchati| khandhapañ‍cakaṃ pana khaṇika dhammattā na gacchati| uppan‍nuppan‍naṃ tattha tatthaiva bhij‍jati| santati vasaina gamanākāra nimittaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| aivaṃ avasavattanaṃ hautīti| hautu tattha tatthaiva bhij‍janaṃ| avicchaidappavattiyā sati, gamanaṃ sampaj‍jatiyaiva| gamanai ca sampaj‍jamānai khaṇikabhij‍janaina dausau natthīti cai| atthi| khaṇikamaraṇañhi sattassa susānabhūmi nāma hauti| antarāyaivā āgatai pakkhalantai vā sati, gacchantau sattau yadā kadāci maraṇaṃ vā nigacchati maraṇa mattaṃ vā dukkhanti| kilaisai nimināti pavattaitīti nimittanti vacanatthau| ‘‘vipal‍lāsanimittaṃ’’ti ca vipal‍lāsa dhammānaṃ vasaina upaṭṭhitaṃ kic‍ca nimittaṃ| taṇhā aiva paṇidhīti  sambandhau| mahantānaṃ ādīnavānaṃ rāsi mahāādīnavarāsi| khandhaisu suñ‍ñatalakkhaṇaṃ nāma ati gambhīraṃ hauti| ñāṇassaiva visayabhūtaṃ| suñ‍ñata dassanañ‍ca diṭṭhiyā ujuppaṭipakkhanti āha ‘‘saṅkhāraisu suñ‍ñatadassanavasainā’’tiādiṃ| tattha ‘‘attha siddhi nāmā’’ti maggaphalappaṭilābhasaṅkhātassa atthassa siddhi nāma| attāti abhinivaisau attābhinivaisau| gaṇhātīti gāhau| upādīyatīti atthau| diṭṭhisaṅkhātau gāhau diṭṭhiggāhau| atthābhinivaisau ca sau diṭṭhiggāhau cāti viggahau| tassa paṭipakkhaṃ anattānupassanāñāṇaṃ| anu anu bhavanaṃ ānubhāvau| paramparatau vaḍḍhananti atthau| ‘‘tathā samanupassitvā’’ti suñ‍ñatā kāraina samanupassitvā| vimuñ‍cantassa ariyapuggalassa| ‘‘anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ nāma nāti gambhīraṃ hauti| saddhāyapi visayabhūtaṃ| un‍natilakkhaṇau ca ahaṃ mānau khandhaisu nic‍cadhuvavipal‍lāsamūlakau saddhāya ca paṭipakkhauti āha ‘‘anic‍cadassanavasainā’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘nimittābhinivaisabhūtassā’’ti nic‍cadhuvanimittābhinivaisabhūtassa| ‘‘mānaggāhassā’’ti mānasaṅkhātassa gāhassa| ‘‘saddhāya pī’’tipisaddaina ñāṇaṃ sampiṇḍaiti| dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ nāma bhayasaṃvaiga janakaṃ hauti| ārammaṇaisu taṇhālaulassa taṇhāvipphandanassa paṭipakkhaṃ| visaisatau samādhissa anurūpanti āha ‘‘dukkhadassana vasainā’’tiādiṃ| ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ’’ti pañ‍ñindriya saddhindriya samādhindriya saṅkhātānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ indriya dhammānaṃ| ‘‘vipassanā gamanavasainai vā’’ti vuṭṭhānagāmini vipassanā saṅkhātassa āgamana kāraṇassa vasainaiva|

idāni pubbai yaṃ vuttaṃ ‘sattaariya puggalavibhāgassa pac‍cayabhāvapattiyā cā’ti| tattha satta ariyapuggalavibhāgaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘tatthā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| dhammānusārī nāmāti aittha ‘‘dhammau’’ti pañ‍ñā vuc‍cati ‘sac‍caṃ, dhammau, dhīti, cāgau,ti aittha viya| tikkhataraṃ dhammaṃ anussaratīti dhammānusārī| pañ‍ñābalānusāraina ādimhi maggaṃ labhatīti vuttaṃ hauti| athavā| ‘‘dhammau’’ti nissattanij‍jīvaṭṭhaina saṅkhāra dhammau vuc‍cati| suñ‍ñatadassanañ‍ca nāma visaisatau suddhadhamma dassanaṃ nāma| iti suddhadhammaṃ  anussarati, ñāṇagatiyā anugacchatīti dhammānusārī| diṭṭhipattauti aittha diṭṭhināma dassanañ‍ñāṇaṃ vuc‍cati| āditau paṭṭhāya parisuddha dassanasampan‍nattā diṭṭhibalainaiva sukhaina taṃ taṃ maggaphalaṃ pattauti diṭṭhipattau| tainaiva dassana pañ‍ñābalaina kilaisabandhanatau vimuttauti pañ‍ñāvimuttau| saṅkhāra dhammānaṃ khaṇika dhammabhāvai suṭṭhu saddahanaṃ avaic‍cappasādau saddhā nāma| tattha ‘‘avaic‍cappasādau’’ti ñāṇasampayuttappasādau| ‘‘ñāṇaṃ’’ti ca saṅkhāra dhammānaṃ khaṇikabhāvanimittānaṃ dassanañ‍ñāṇaṃ| tādisai hi ñāṇai sati, tattha avaic‍cappasādau nāma jāyati, nau añ‍ñathāti| saddhaṃ anussaratīti saddhānusārī| saddhābalainaiva ādimhi ariyamaggaṃ pāpuṇātīti vuttaṃ hauti| saddhābalaina vimuttauti saddhāvimuttau| ‘‘kāyasakkhi nāmā’’ti aittha sakkhaṃti sandiṭṭhaṃ| yaṃ sādhakantipi vuc‍cati| attanau kāyai aiva laddhaṃ sakkhaṃ yassāti kāyasakkhī| dukkhānu passī puggalau hi saṃvaiga bahulau hauti| saṃvaigau ca padhāna vīriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṃviggau yaunisau padahatīti hi vuttaṃ| tasmā sau saṃvaigavasaina bhāvanaṃ anuyuñ‍jantau yaṃ yaṃ purisathāmaina pattabbaṃ hauti| taṃ taṃ pāpuṇāti| pāpuṇantau ca attanau kāyai aiva sakkhaṃ labhati| sandiṭṭhaṃ jhānasukhaṃ vā maggaphalasukhaṃ vā labhatīti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘ubhataubhāga vimuttau’’ti arūpajjhānaihi rūpakāyatau arahattamaggaphalaihi nāmakāyatauti aivaṃ ubhauhi kāyabhāgaihi vimuttau| aittha ca tayau ariyapuggalā pañ‍ñādhikau ca saddhādhikau ca vīriyādhikau ca| tattha dhammānusārī ca diṭṭhipattau ca pañ‍ñāvimuttau ca pañ‍ñādhikau nāma| saddhānusārī ca saddhāvimuttau ca saddhādhikau nāma| kāyasakkhī ca ubhataubhāgavimuttau ca vīriyādhikau nāmāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ayañ‍ca puggalavibhāganayau paṭisambhidāmagganayaina vuttau| abhidhammai pana puggala pañ‍ñattiyaṃ saddhāvimuttau ca kāyasakkhī ca dvaipuggalā saikkhaisu aiva vuttā| arahattaṃ pattā pana aitai dvaipi pañ‍ñāvimuttā aivāti daṭṭhabbā| aittha siyā, saṅkhāra dhammānaṃ khaṇika dhammabhāvai suṭṭhu saddahanaṃ avaic‍cappasādau saddhā nāmāti vuttaṃ| kiṃ pana tādisaina saddhāmattaina sautāpatti maggaṃ  pāpuṇātīti| aittha amhaihi vattabbaṃ natthi| bhagavatā aiva vuttaṃ| yathāha mahāvaggai auk‍kanta saṃyuttai cakkhu bhikkhavai anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī, yau aivaṃ saddahati, aivaṃ adhimuc‍cati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati saddhānusārī| auk‍kantau ariyabhūmiṃ| atik‍kantau puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ| sau yainakammaina apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya| na taṃ kammaṃ karautīti| sautaṃ bhikkhavai anic‍caṃ| vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvītiādinā dvādasāyatanāni vitthārai tabbāni| tattha ‘‘vipariṇāmī’’ti vipariṇāmau yassa atthīti vipariṇāmī| vipariṇāmauti ca khaṇai khaṇai jīraṇatā bhij‍janatā vuc‍cati| ‘‘añ‍ñathābhāvī’’ti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ adhimuc‍catī’’ti aitaina ñāṇasampayuttasaddhaṃ dīpaiti| sā aiva sautāpattiyaṅgaisu buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, dhammai, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatauti vuttā| tattha ñāṇasampayuttasaddhā nāma cakkhādīnaṃ anic‍canimitta dassanañ‍ñāṇaina sampayuttā aukappanasaddhā| anic‍canimittañ‍ca nāma rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ khayaṭṭhainātipadai vuttanayaina cakkhādinissayānaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ santati parivattanādikaṃ vuc‍cati| tañhi passantau cakkhādīnaṃ anic‍cabhāvai saddahati, adhimuc‍cati| tānipi khaṇai khaṇai aikantaina bhij‍jati yaivāti saddhādhimaukkhaṃ saddhāvinicchayaṃ paṭilabhatīti atthau| ‘‘buddhai avaic‍cappasādainā’’ti aittha pana arahatā sammāsambuddhatādīnaṃ buddhaguṇādīnaṃ dassanañ‍ñāṇaina sampayuttau pasādau avaic‍cappasādau nāma| ariyabhūmi nāma diṭṭhānusaya vicikicchānusayaihi parisuddhā avatthābhūmi vuc‍cati| taihi sahitā avatthābhūmi puthuj‍janabhūmi nāma| kathaṃ pana tassa puggalassa puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ atik‍kantatā ariyabhūmiṃ auk‍kantatā ca viñ‍ñāyatīti āha ‘‘sau yainā’’tiādiṃ| yau attanau jīvitahaitupi kiñ‍ci kammapathapattaṃ apāyagāmi kammaṃ na karauti| sau aikantaina puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ atik‍kantau, ariyabhūmiṃ auk‍kantauti viñ‍ñāyatīti vuttaṃ hauti| idaṃ saddhānusārisuttaṃ| aparaṃpi vuttaṃ cakkhu bhikkhavai vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yassa aivaṃ mattasau nijjhānaṃ khamati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati dhammānusārī tiādi| tattha ‘‘mattasau  nijjhānaṃ khamatī’’ti saddhānusārīviya saddahanamattai aṭṭhatvā cakkhādīnaṃ tādisaṃ anic‍cataṃ samanupassantau thaukaṃ thaukaṃ dassanaṃ labhatiyaiva| aivaṃsati, cakkhādīni tassa puggalassa nijjhānaṃ aulaukanaṃ mattasau khamanti nāma| idaṃ dhammānusārisuttaṃ| aparaṃpi vuttaṃ cakkhu bhikkhavai anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau aivaṃ pajānāti passati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati sautāpan‍nau tiādi| tattha ‘‘pajānāti passatī’’ti dhammānusārīviya mattasau nijjhānakkhamamattai aṭṭhatvā pañ‍ñāya pajānāti, ñāṇacakkhunau pac‍cakkhatau passatīti atthau| idaṃ diṭṭhipattādīnaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘yathā sakaṃ phalaṃ’’ti aittha sassa attanau idaṃ sakaṃ| yaṃ yaṃ sakaṃ yathāsakanti atthaṃ dassaiti sautāpan‍nassātiādinā| phalasamāpatti vīthiyaṃ pana anulaumajavanāni aiva phalānaṃ āsan‍nakāraṇāni, na maggajavanānīti āha ‘‘na maggāgamanavasainā’’ti| ‘‘sau’’ti maggau| santau saṃvij‍jamānau sabbhāvau| ‘‘assā’’ti maggassa|

anic‍cānupassanā kic‍catau animittā hauti| sabhāvatau sanimittāaiva| nic‍canimittasahitāaiva| yatau sā aisauhamasmītiggahaṇaṃ labhati| idaṃpi vipassanāñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ khayaṭṭhaināti samanupassitabbā ca hautīti| tattha ca abhidhammaṃ patvā sabhāvappadhānaṃ hauti, na kic‍cappadhānaṃ| tasmā abhidhamma nayaina vipassanā attanau nimitta nāmaṃ maggassa dātuṃ na sak‍kauti| ayaṃ aṭṭhakathāya adhippāyau| kilaisa dhammā nāma yassa uppaj‍janti, taṃ puggalaṃ palibundhanti| sambādhaṃ karaunti| tasmā palibaudhakarānāma| taṃ puggalaṃ nic‍canimitta subhanimitta sukhanimitta attanimittāni gāhainti| tasmā nimittakarānāma| taṃ puggalaṃ ārammaṇaisu bhusaṃ nihitaṃ niviṭṭhaṃ karaunti| tasmā paṇidhikarānāma| tai ca maggaphalāni ārammaṇaṃ na karaunti| maggaphalaihi ca sampayaugaṃ na gacchanti| aivaṃ ārammaṇakaraṇā divasaina maggaphalaisu taisaṃ abhāvau| abhāvattāaiva ca tāni maggaphalāni attanau dhammatāya tīṇināmāni labhantīti dassaituṃ ‘‘ārammaṇa karaṇavasainavā’’tiādi  vuttaṃ| ‘‘attanau sabhāvainā’’ti attanau dhammatāya|

‘‘attadiṭṭhiyā uppan‍nāya uppaj‍jantī’’ti idaṃ tāsaṃ diṭṭhivicikicchānaṃ attadiṭṭhiyā avinābhāvaṃ dīpaiti| na pana attadiṭṭhiyā tāhi avinābhāvanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| vipac‍cantīti apāyaisu vipac‍canti| apāyapaṭisandhiṃ janainti| diṭṭhasac‍cānañ‍ca ariyapuggalānaṃ| diṭṭhivicikicchāsampayuttā akusaladhammā diṭṭhivicikicchāggahaṇaina gahitāti āha ‘‘diṭṭhivicikicchā vippayuttā’’ti| ‘‘akusaladhammācai vā’’ti pac‍cuppan‍nabhūtā dasākusalakammapatha dhammā caiva| ‘‘sattahi bhavaihī’’ti kriyāpavaggai karaṇavacanaṃ| yathā sattahi māsaihi vihāraṃ niṭṭhāpaitīti| kriyāpavaggautica kriyāyasīghataraṃ niṭṭhāpanaṃ vuc‍cati| ‘‘sattakkhattuṃ’’ti sattavārā| tīṇi saṃyaujanānināma ‘sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsasaṃyaujanāni,| ‘‘avinipāta dhammau’’ti virūpaṃ hutvā patanaṃ vinipātau| apāyanipātauti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘dhammau’’ti sabhāvau| natthi vinipātau dhammau yassāti viggahau| ‘‘niyatau’’ti sugatibhavaisu niyata gatikau| sammā bujjhatīti sambaudhi| maggañ‍ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘parāyanaṃ’’ti parabhāgai gantabbaṭṭhānaṃ vuc‍cati| sambaudhisaṅkhātaṃ parāyanaṃ assāti sambaudhiparāyanau| ‘‘aniyamatthau’’ti vāsaddatthauti vuttaṃ hauti| vā saddatthaisu ca aniyamatthau| na vikappatthau| vikappatthaihi sati dvai aiva sattakkhattuparamā labbhanti| missakabhavauna labbhati| aniyamatthai tayaupi labbhanti| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana samuc‍cayatthassa gahitattā aikau missakabhavau aiva labbhati| nanu ca saddau nāma samuc‍cayatthai pākaṭau, na aniyamatthai| tasmā samuc‍cayatthauva idha yuttauti cai| na yuttau| kasmā| dvin‍naṃ asaṅgahitattā| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana samuc‍cayatthaṃ gahaitvā tai dvai puggalā imasmiṃ pāṭhai asaṅgahitāti vuttaṃ| yadi tai imasmiṃ pāṭhai asaṅgahitā| katthatai saṅgahitā bhavaiyyuṃ| na hi kauci sattakkhattu paramau nāma idha asaṅgahitauti yuttauti| ‘‘assā’’ti tassapāḷipāṭhassa| ‘‘aittha ca yasmā| la| paramau nāma vuttau’’ti idaṃ upari ‘yai kāmabhavai yaivā’tiādinā  ‘yaidiṭṭha dhammai vā’tiādinā ca yaujaitabbaṃ| sau ca sukkhavipassakau vā parihīnajjhānau vā daṭṭhabbauti sambandhau| ‘‘pāḷiyaṃ’’ti puggalapañ‍ñatti pāḷiyaṃ| ‘‘idha niṭṭhā’’ti imasmiṃ kāmalaukai āsavakkhayaṃ patvā khandhaparinibbānapatti vasaina niṭṭhā| niṭṭhānaṃ pariyausānanti atthau| kulatau kulaṃ gacchatīti kaulaṃkaulau| bhavatau bhavaṃ gacchatīti atthau| aikamaiva paṭisandhisaṅkhātaṃ bhavabījaṃ assa atthīti aikabījī| yau ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai arahā| tassa ca idha niṭṭhāti yaujanā| kaina pana tiṇṇaṃ sautāpan‍nānaṃ visaisau katauti| sattakkhattu paramau tāva kaivalaṃ pathamamagga sampayuttaihi indriyaihi katau| itarai dvai uparimaggatthāya pavattitaihi vipassanindriyaihi katauti| tattha sattakkhattu paramau sabbamudukau hauti| tatau kaulaṃkaulau tikkhau| sau pana cha bhavikau, pañ‍cabhavikau, catubhavikau, tibhavikau, dvibhavikauti pañ‍cavidhau| tattha pañ‍cabhavikau tikkhau| saisā anuk‍kamaina tikkhatarāti daṭṭhabbā| aikabījī pana aikabhavikau sabbatikkhau| tattha sattakkhattu paramau kaivalaina pathamamaggānubhāvaina siddhattā sabbamudukau hauti| itarai dvaipakatiyā sattakkhattu paramabhāvai ṭhatvā uparimaggatthāya vipassanā kammaṃ paṭṭhapainti| tadā taisaṃ indriyāni puna visaisa pattāni haunti, tikkhāni| tasmā tai sattakkhattu paramatau tikkhānāma hauntīti| ‘‘sattavāratau auraṃ vā’’ti idaṃ kaulaṃkaula aikabījinau sandhāya vuttaṃ| ‘‘upari tiṇṇaṃ maggānaṃ vipassanā niyāmaitī’’ti idaṃ uparimai dvaisautāpan‍nai sandhāya vuttaṃ| sattakkhattu paramaupana pathamamaggainaiva niyamitauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| tathāhi sauyaiva saṅgahapāṭhai sarūpatau āgatau sautāpatti maggaṃ bhāvaitvā diṭṭhivicikicchāpahānaina pahīnāpāyagamanau sattakkhattu paramau sautāpan‍nau nāma hautīti| itarai pana dvai pāḷiyaṃ sautāpan‍na puggalavibhāgaṭṭhānaisvaiva āgatāti| ‘‘yai panā’’ti yai sautāpan‍na puggalāpana| gāthāyaṃ ‘‘itausattā’’ti imasmiṃ manussalaukai satta saṃsarāni| ‘‘tatausattā’’ti tasmiṃ daivalaukai  sattāti atthau| aṅguttarapāṭhai ‘‘tainacittappasādainā’’ti mahāsahassilaukadhātuṃ saraina viñ‍ñāpaituṃ samatthassa satthuguṇai cittappasādau jāyati| taina cittappasādaina| ‘‘taina pariyāyainā’’ti sacai sau daivaisu aiva saṃsarati| sattakkhattuṃ aupapātika paṭisandhiyau gaṇhitvā tatthaiva parinibbāyissati| yadi manussaisu aiva saṃsarati| sattagabbhasaiyyaka paṭisandhiyau gaṇhitvā aitthaiva parinibbāyissati| aivaṃ daisanā vāraina cuddasa katvā daisitaina pariyāyaināti atthau| ‘‘taṃ vīmaṃsitabbaṃ’’ti vadantaina ṭīkācariyaina sayaṃ cuddasapaṭisandhiyauva icchatīti dīpaiti| idāni taṃ cuddasapaṭisandhivādaṃ paṭikkhipantau ‘‘vibhaṅgai panā’’tiādimāha| ‘‘vibhaṅgai’’ti abhidhammai ñāṇavibhaṅgai| natthi ṭhānamassāti aṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘ṭhānaṃ’’ti mūlakāraṇaṃ| natthi avakāsau assāti anavakāsau| ‘‘avakāsau’’ti pac‍cayakāraṇaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ’’ti kriyāparāmasanaṃ| yaṃ nibbattaiyya, taṃ nibbattanaṃ aṭṭhānaṃ| sau nibbattana dhammau anavakāsauti yaujanā| ‘‘abhisaṅkhāra viñ‍ñāṇassā’’ti sattabhavatau uddhaṃ kāmabhavai paṭisandhijanakassa kusalā kusalakamma viñ‍ñāṇassa| ‘‘niraudhainā’’ti sautāpatti maggakkhaṇai aiva niraudhanaina| nirujjhantīti sambandhau| ‘‘nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍cā’’ti yai nāma rūpadhammā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ| aitai nāma rūpadhammā aittha aitasmiṃ abhisaṅkhāra viñ‍ñāṇa niraudhakkhaṇai nirujjhantīti yaujanā| ‘‘yathāvuttanayainaivā’’ti taina pariyāyaināti vuttappakāranayainaiva| ‘‘tadaikaṭṭhamauhassā’’ti taina rāgaina taina dausaina aikasmiṃ cittai aikatau ṭhitassa mauhassa| na diṭṭhivicikicchā sampayuttassa mauhassa| tassa pathamamaggai aiva pahīnattā| nāpi uddhac‍cacittasampayuttassa mauhassa| tassa catutthamaggai aiva pahātabbattā| uppaj‍jantā rāgadausamauhā| ‘‘dhammikaisū’’ti dhammaina samaina uppan‍naisu| ‘‘yatau’’ti yaṃkāraṇā| balavantakāraṇāti atthau| ‘‘taisaṃ’’ti sautāpan‍nānaṃ| ‘‘sal‍likhana vasainā’’ti bahalapakkhaṃ thūlabhāgaṃ suṭṭhulikhanavasaina| adhic‍cuppatti nāma pubbai yathāpavattaṃ kālaṃ adhic‍ca atik‍kamma uppatti| uppaj‍jantānampi rāgadausamauhānaṃ| ‘‘taisaṃ pī’’ti sakadāgāmīnampi| ‘‘maithunavatthu samāyaugau icchitau’’ti jāyampatīnaṃ maithunakammasamāyaugau nāma sakadāgāmīnaṃ atthīti icchitau| thairauhi adhic‍cuppattimattaṃ icchati| pariyuṭṭhānamandataṃ na icchati| sakadāgāmīnaṃ puttadhītaraupi sandissantīti vadati| ‘‘mahāaṭṭhakathāyaṃ paṭikkhittau’’ti thairassa manaurathamattaṃti vatvā paṭikkhittau| idaṃ sabbaṃ aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ vuttaṃ| ‘‘chak‍kanipātai’’ti aṅguttarai chak‍kanipātai| ‘‘migāsāḷavatthumhī’’ti migāsāḷanāmikāya upāsikāya vatthumhi| kaici imaṃ vatthuṃ disvā mahāaṭṭhakathāvādaṃ na raucainti| mahāsivatthairavādaṃ raucainti| ‘‘abrahmacārinau’’ti maithuna dhammā appaṭiviratassa| ‘‘sakadāgāmittaṃ byākataṃ’’ti tasmiṃ kālaṅkatai sau brāhmaṇau idha sakadāgāmibhāvaṃ patvā tusitākāyaṃ upapan‍nauti aivaṃ tassa yaṃ sakadāgāmittaṃ bhagavatā byākataṃ| ‘‘adhigamavasainā’’ti sakadāgāmibhāvaṃ paṭilābhavasaina| na pana abrahmacārinau satau adhigamavasaināti adhippāyau| maraṇāsan‍nakālaipi hi sikkhāpadādhiṭṭhānaina saha uparimaggaphalādhigamanaṃ nāma tikkhapañ‍ñassa ariyasāvakassa na duk‍karanti| aitaina kaisañ‍ci thairavādaraucanaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ hauti| ‘‘sakiṃdaiva imaṃlaukaṃ āgantvā’’ti aitaina itau paralaukaṃ gamanassa ca tatau puna idhāgamanassa ca siddhattā ayaṃ puggalau suddhaina magganiyāmaina kāmabhavai dvikkhattu paramaunāma hautīti siddhau| kathaṃ| idha patvā tattha nibbattitvā idha parinibbāyī, tattha patvā idha nibbattitvā tattha parinibbāyīti| saisāpana cattārau vipassanāniyāmaina siddhā hauntīti viñ‍ñāyati| katamai cattārau| idha patvā idha parinibbāyī| tattha patvā tattha parinibbāyī| idha patvā tattha parinibbāyī| tattha patvā idha parinibbāyīti| aittha ca tīsu sautāpan‍naisu aikausattakkhattu paramau aiva magganiyāmaina siddhau| saisā pana dvai sautāpan‍nā vipassanāniyāmaina siddhā| cha sakadāgāmīsu dvai aiva magganiyāmaina siddhā| saisā pana cattārau vipassanāniyāmaina siddhā| tattha maggasiddhaisu sautāpan‍na sakadāgāmīsu  saṅkarau natthi| vipassanāsiddhaisu pana saṅkarau atthi| atthatau aviruddhai sati, vauhāramattaina saṅkarai dausau natthīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| āgantvāti pāṭhavasaina āgamanatauti vuttaṃ| āgantāti pana pāṭhau yuttau| āgamanasīlau āgamana dhammau hutvā sakadāgāmī nāmāti atthau| anāgantvā itthattanti aitthapi aisaivanayau| arahatau bhāvau arahattaṃ| arahatta maggaṃ| dakkhiṇāya vipattikarānāma appapphala appānisaṃsakarā| sampattikarā nāma mahapphala mahānisaṃsakarā| sīlakkhandhādiguṇā nāma sīlakkhandha samādhikkhandha pañ‍ñākkhandha vimuttikkhandha vimuttiñ‍ñāṇa dassanakkhandhaguṇā| ‘‘dakkhiṇaiyyaisū’’ti dakkhiṇāraha puggalaisu| ‘‘dhuraṃ’’ti padhānasīsaṭṭhāniyaṃ vuc‍cati| saddhā aiva dhuraṃ assāti saddhādhurau| aivaṃ pañ‍ñādhurau| kasmā panaittha vīriyadhurau na gahitauti| pāḷiyaṃ saddhāvāhī maggaṃ bhāvaiti, pañ‍ñāvāhī maggaṃ bhāvaitīti vuttaṃ| na pana vuttaṃ vīriyavāhī maggaṃ bhāvaitīti| tasmā na gahitauti| kasmā pana vīriyavāhī maggaṃ bhāvaitīti na vuttanti| vuc‍catai| maggaṃ bhāvaintassa nāma ādimhi diṭṭhivicikicchānaṃ pahānāya kammaṃ mahantaṃ hauti| kasmā, apāyamūlakattā| tattha saddhā ca vicikicchā ca dvai ujuppaṭipakkhā haunti| yasmiṃ ārammaṇai saddhā nivisati, tasmiṃ vicikicchā natthi| yasmiṃ vicikicchā nivisati, tasmiṃ saddhā natthi| tathā pañ‍ñādiṭṭhiyau ca ujuppaṭipakkhā| yattha pañ‍ñā, na tattha diṭṭhi| yattha diṭṭhi, na tattha pañ‍ñāti| api ca saddhā attanau visayai diṭṭhiṃ vidhamatiyaiva| pañ‍ñāya vicikicchāvidhamanai vattabbamaiva natthi| tasmā āraddha vipassakau anic‍cādīsu avaic‍cappasādamattainapi diṭṭhivicikicchāyau pajahitvā saddhānusāribhāvañ‍ca suṭṭhutaraṃ pasīditvā saddhāvimuttabhāvañ‍ca pāpuṇāti| pañ‍ñāya anic‍cādīni nijjhānakkhamaṃ katvā dhammānusāribhāvañ‍ca suṭṭhutaraṃ passitvā diṭṭhipattapañ‍ñā vimuttabhāvañ‍ca pāpuṇāti| vīriyaṃ pana diṭṭhivicikicchānaṃ paṭipakkhamattampi na hauti| atha khau tāsaṃ sahāyaupi hauti, dhurabhūtañ‍ca| paggahalakkhaṇañhi vīriyaṃ| taṃ saddhaṃpi paggaṇhāti, pañ‍ñaṃpi paggaṇhāti, diṭṭhiṃpi paggaṇhāti, vicikicchampi paggaṇhātiyaiva| kutaupana attanau sabhāvaina diṭṭhivicikicchāyau  pajahituṃ sakkhissati| tasmā imasmiṃ ṭhānai vīriyavāhīti ca vīriyadhurauti ca na vuttauti| kāyasakkhimhi pana saddhā ca pañ‍ñā ca samabalā haunti| vīriyañ‍ca sayaṃ sammappadhānaṭṭhānai ṭhatvā tadubhayaṃ upabrūhaiti| sau ca puggalau tadubhayabalaina diṭṭhivicikicchādayau pajahitvā kāyasakkhibhāvaṃ pāpuṇāti| aṭṭhavimaukkhai vā uppādaitvā ubhataubhāga vimuttabhāvaṃ pāpuṇāti| imaisu pana dvīsu puggalaisu vīriyassa thāmau suṭṭhu pākaṭau| tasmā imai dvai vīriyādhikātvaiva vattabbā| na vīriyadhurauti| catuppaṭipadā nāma dukkhāpaṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā| dukkhāpaṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| sukhāpaṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā| sukhāpaṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| tividhavimaukkhau nāma suñ‍ñatau vimaukkhau| animittau vimaukkhau| appaṇihitau vimaukkhau| ‘‘tayau antarā parinibbāyinau’’ti aittha āyukappassa pariyantaṃ apatvā antarā aiva kilaisaparinibbānapattā antarāparinibbāyī nāma| sau tividhau| āyukappassa pathamabhāgai parinibbāyī, dutīyabhāgai, tatīyabhāgaiti| imai tayau antarā parinibbāyinau nāma| upahac‍caparinibbāyī nāma āyukappa pariyantai parinibbāyī| uddhaṃ mukhau vaṭṭasautau aitassāti uddhaṃsautau| akaniṭṭhaṃ avassaṃ gamissatīti akaniṭṭhagāmī| vipassanā kammaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappayaugaṃ katvā parinibbāyī sasaṅkhāra parinibbāyī| saṅkhārarahitaina sukhaina vipassanā kammaina parinibbāyī asaṅkhāra parinibbāyī| ‘‘dvādasa sautāpan‍nā’’ti aikabījikā tayau| tai aiva catūhi paṭipadāhi guṇitā dvādasa| dvādasa sakadāgāminau pubbai vuttanayā aiva|

aikac‍cānaṃ aiva anāgāmi arahantānaṃ aiva| ‘‘ajjhausitassā’’ti adhiausitassa| mamaividanti niṭṭhānaṃ katvā ṭhapitassa| gilitvā ṭhapitassāti vuttaṃ hauti| bahalaina thūlaina kāmarāgaina vimuttā bahalarāgavimuttā| āruppaisu ca tassau arūpasamāpattiyau aiva atthi| na catassau rūpasamāpattiyau| tasmā tattha anupubbaniraudhau na labbhati| ‘‘āditau paṭṭhāyā’’ti aṭṭhasu samāpattīsu pathamajjhāna samāpattiādi| tatau paṭṭhāya| anupubbaniraudhau nāma  pathamajjhānai paṭighasañ‍ñānānattasañ‍ñānaṃ niraudhau| paṭighasañ‍ñā nāma pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇasañ‍ñā| nānattasañ‍ñā nāma kasiṇa nimittatau añ‍ñaisu nānārammaṇaisu pavattamanauviñ‍ñāṇa sañ‍ñā| dutīyajjhānai vitak‍ka vicārānaṃ niraudhautiādinā anupubba niraudhau| ‘‘tatau pañ‍camajjhānaṃ’’ti pañ‍cakanayaina vuttaṃ| aṭṭhasamāpatti vacanaṃ pana catuk‍kanayaina vuttaṃ| tadubhayaṃpi puggalānurūpaṃ labbhatiyaiva| yuganandhaṃ nāma dvandabandhanaṃ, samathavipassanānaṃ yuganandhanti viggahau| ‘‘samāpaj‍jana vuṭṭhāna vipassanāvasainā’’ti aittha vasibhāvapattaṃ samāpaj‍janañ‍ca vuṭṭhānañ‍ca gahaitabbaṃ| attanau sarīrā baddhaisu parikkhāraisu samāpattibalainaiva antarāyābhāvatau visuṃ adhiṭṭhātabba kic‍caṃ natthīti vuttaṃ ‘‘attanau sarīrābaddhai parikkhārai ṭhapaitvā’’ti| ‘‘nānābaddhānī’’ti nānāṭhānaihi ābaddhāni| nānāṭhānaisu ṭhapitānīti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘parissayainā’’ti antarāyaina| māvinassantūti vā nānābaddha avikaupanaṃ adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ| tadā vuṭṭhahāmītivā saṅghappatimānanaṃ adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ| aivaṃ satthupak‍kausanaṃpi| ‘‘addhānaparicchaidau’’ti attanau jīvitakālassa upaparikkhaṇaṃ| maraṇañhi nāma samāpattibalainapi paṭibāhituṃ na sak‍kāhauti| antau samāpattiyañ‍ca maraṇai sati sabrahmacārīsu kattabbavattaṃ na karaiyya| aitañhi vattaṃ yaṃ ariyasāvakānaṃ maraṇāsan‍nai sabrahmacārīnaṃ vā upaṭṭhākānaṃ vā appamādakathānu sāsanikammaṃ| samāpattiyaṃ vā añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ garahā vivaj‍janatthaṃ| aivañhi tai garahaiyyuṃ| gautamasāvakānaṃ niraudhasamāpatti nāma aikaṃ maraṇamukhaṃ| yaṃ samāpaj‍jantau asukau bhikkhu maraṇaṃ gacchati| anariya kammaṃ haitanti|

‘‘yadatthaṃ’’ti paṭipatti rasassādatthaṃ| ‘‘tadatthai’’ti tasmiṃ atthai| nirāmisa sukhaṃ nāma āmisarahita sukhaṃ| duvidhañhi sukhaṃ sāmisasukhañ‍ca nirāmisasukhañ‍ca| tattha yai laukai iṭṭha kanta manāpiyā pañ‍cakāmaguṇā nāma atthi| tai kilaisaihi āmasitabbattā āmisā nāma| yañ‍casukhaṃ tai āmisai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati, taṃ sāmisasukhaṃ nāma| yaṃ laukai dibbaṃ sukhaṃ mānusakaṃ sukhanti mamāyanti| yañ‍ca anubhavantaṃ  mahājanaṃ sabbāni apāyabhaya vaṭṭabhayāni samparivārainti| jhānasukha maggasukha phalasukha nibbānasukhaṃ pana nirāmisasukhaṃ nāma| yaṃ naikkhammasukhanti ca pavivaikasukhanti ca upasamasukhanti ca sambaudhisukhanti ca vimuttisukhanti ca ariyasukhanti ca anavaj‍jasukhanti ca vuc‍cati| yañ‍ca anubhavantaṃ ariyajanaṃ sabbāni apāyabhaya vaṭṭabhayāni samparivaj‍janti| ‘‘assāditabbaṭṭhainā’’ti kilaisapalibaudhaihi vimuttattā vipula gambhīra santa paṇītarasattā ca ahausukhaṃ ahausukhanti udāharantainapi assāditabbaṭṭhaināti|

kammaṭṭhānasaṅgahānudīpanā niṭṭhitā|

nigamagāthāsu|

262. caritabbanti cārittaṃ| dasakusalakammapathadhammajātaṃ| ‘‘kulācārainā’’ti kulaparamparatau āgataina ācāraina| ‘‘ñāti mitta dhana bhaugasampattiyā’’ti haituatthai karaṇavacanaṃ| ‘‘visālai’’ti vipulai| ‘‘tainā’’ti tassapadassa vibhatyanta dassanaṃ| ‘‘saddhāyā’’ti ratanattayai avaic‍cappasādasaddhāvasaina| ‘‘abhivuḍḍhānaṃ’’ti hānabhāgiya ṭhitibhāgiyabhāvaṃ atik‍kammavisaisabhāgiya nibbaidhabhāgiya bhāvapattivasaina atissaya vuḍḍhānaṃ| ‘‘parisuddhānañ‍cā’’ti attuk‍kaṃsana paravambhanādi dausaihi parisuddhānañ‍ca| naṃ naṃ janaṃ pātirakkhatīti nampau| diṭṭhadiṭṭhaisu maittāvihārīti vuttaṃ hauti| āvhātabbauti avhayau| nāmaṃ| nampau iti avhayau assāti nampavhayauti vacanatthau| ‘‘paṇidhāyā’’ti patthaitvāti atthaṃ nivattaiti ‘‘bhusaṃ nidhāyā’’tiādinā| ‘‘upādāya paṭic‍cā’’ti tassa padassa pariyāyadassanamaitaṃ| ‘‘attanau mandabuddhīnaṃ’’ti pac‍cakkhai dharamānānanti adhippāyau| taināha ‘‘pacchima janānaṃ vā’’ti| anukampanaṃ anukampau| kathaṃ nu khau imai janā abhidhammatthaisu sukhaina ājānaiyyunti aivaṃ pavattau cittabyāpārau| atthatau anudayasaṅkhātā karuṇāyaiva| taināha ‘‘anudayaṃ  kāruñ‍ñaṃ’’ti| ‘‘patthitaṃ’’ti vā sādhuvatassa yaṃ thairau aivarūpaṃ pakaraṇaṃ karaiyyāti aivaṃ cirakālaṃ patthitaṃ āsiṃsitanti atthau| ‘‘khuddasikkhāṭīkāyaṃ’’ti paurāṇaṭīkaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| saumavihārautvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha tasmiṃ dīpaiti adhippāyau| gāthāyaṃ ‘‘athā’’ti tasmiṃkālai| ‘‘aitthā’’ti aitasmiṃ nagarai| ‘‘saddau’’ti kasmā rājā aivarūpaṃ maṅgalahatthiṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ adāsi| naitaṃ patirūpaṃ yaṃ rañ‍ñā katanti aivaṃ pavattau amanāpasaddau| ‘‘bhairavau’’ti bhīrutājanakau bhayānakau| ‘‘sivīnaṃ’’ti siviraṭṭhavāsīnaṃ| ‘‘raṭṭhavaḍḍhanai’’ti raṭṭhaṃ vaḍḍhaitīti raṭṭhavaḍḍhanau| hatthināgau| sau hi lakkhaṇasampan‍nau ājāniyau hauti| yasmiṃ raṭṭhai taṃ abhimaṅgalaṃ karaunti, tasmiṃ raṭṭhai daivau sammā vassati, nānābhayupaddavā tasmiṃ nuppaj‍jantīti laukasammatau maṅgalahatthī hauti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanai’’ti| ‘‘vitthiṇṇau’’ti vitthāritau| ativitthārauti vuttaṃ hauti| dhanīyanti mahājanaihi daṭṭhuṃ patthīyantīti dhañ‍ñā| mahiddhikā| adhivasanti aitthāti adhivāsau| dhañ‍ñānaṃ adhivāsauti samāsau| ajjhāvasiṃsu aitthāti ajjhāvutthaṃ| kittiguṇavasaina udainti uggacchantīti uditā| uditānaṃpi uditauti viggahau| taināha ‘‘nabhamajjhai’’tiādiṃ| sāraj‍janaṃ sāradau| vigatau sāradau yassāti visāradau| visāradassa bhāvau vaisāraj‍jaṃ| pariyattiyaṃ vaisāraj‍jaṃ pariyattivaisāraj‍jaṃ| taipiṭakapariyattiyaṃ vaiyyattiyañ‍ñāṇaṃ| taṃ ādi aitissā pañ‍ñāyāti pariyattivaisāraj‍jādi pañ‍ñā| pāpatau laj‍janasīlāti laj‍jinauti āha ‘‘pāpagarahinau’’ti| pāpaṃ dhammaṃ garahantiādīnavaṃ kathayanti sīlaināti pāpagarahinau| ‘‘anussarantū’’ti punappunaṃ sarantu| visaisaina bhavanti vaipul‍laṃ gacchantīti vibhavā| puñ‍ñāni ca tāni vibhavācāti puñ‍ñavibhavā| vipulapuñ‍ñānīti dassaituṃ ‘‘vipulānaṃ’’tiādimāha| anuttariya dhammā nāma dassanānuttariya savanānuttariyādayau| laukai bahūsu dassanaisu itau uttaraṃ añ‍ñaṃ dassanaṃ nāma natthīti dassanānuttariyaṃ| ratanattayadassanaṃ| catusac‍cadassanañ‍ca| ratanattayaguṇakathāsavanaṃ, khandhāyatana dhātu sac‍capaṭic‍casamuppādakathāsavanañ‍ca savanānuttariyaṃ nāma| taisu ratanattayādīsu saddhā paṭilābhau lābhānuttariyaṃ nāma| taisamaiva punappunaṃ saraṇaṃ anussatānuttariyaṃ nāma| ratanattayai catūhi pac‍cayaihi vā vattappaṭivattakaraṇaina vā paricaraṇaṃ pāricariyānuttariyaṃ nāma| buddhapañ‍ñattāsu tīsu sikkhāsu samādāya sikkhanaṃ sikkhānuttariyaṃ nāma| tādisai pañ‍ñāvadātaguṇasaubhādikai guṇai avassaṃ bhajanti pāpuṇantīti tādisaguṇabhāginau| ‘‘bhavissāmā’’ti bhavituṃ ussāhaṃ karissāmāti vuttaṃ hauti|

dīpaniyā nigamagāthāsu|

‘‘bashyusuñ‍ñacammaikamhī’’ti aittha ‘‘bashyū’’ti aṭṭhasaṅkhyāya saṅkaita vacanaṃ| ‘‘cammaṃ’’ti dvisaṅkhyāya| aikañ‍ca dukañ‍ca suñ‍ñañ‍ca aṭṭhacāti gaṇanak‍kamau| sahassa dvisata aṭṭhasaṅkhātai sākaiti atthau| aisanayau paratau ‘‘navapañ‍ca cammaikamhī’’ti padaipi| gāthāvaṇṇanāsu| nagarati na cavatīti nagaraṃ| thāvariyanti vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘rañ‍jaitī’’ti ramaiti| khaimasubhikkhatādiguṇaihi anāgatai janai gamāpaiti, āgatai añ‍ñattha gantuṃ na daitīti vuttaṃ hauti| rauhitamigā nāma suvaṇṇavaṇṇamigā| ‘‘sākai’’ti sākanāmaina rañ‍ñā puna saṅkhatattā imaṃ kaliyugaṃ sākanti vuc‍cati| yaṃ aitarahi sākrarājantipi vauharanti| sākarājā ca nāma uttarāpathaisu aikau gandhārarājā| yaṃ jaiṭṭhakaṃ katvā bhin‍naladdhikā vaj‍jībhikkhū imaṃ dhamma vinayaṃ mahāsaṅghīkaṃ nāma saṅgahaṃ āraupainti| sau ca dhammavinayasaṅgahau uttarāpathaisu vattati| vaisāliyaṃ yasatthaira saṅgahau pana dakkhiṇāpathaisūti| cattāriyugāni nāma laukai pañ‍ñapainti satyayugaṃ, dvāparayugaṃ, taitrayugaṃ, kaliyuga,nti| yuganti ca āyuparimāṇaṃ vuc‍cati| idānipana kaliyugaṃ vattati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sākaiti kaliyugai’’ti| pāpayugaiti atthau| ‘‘la tī’’ti sālikkhaittānaṃ saudhanaṭṭhānattā aivaṃ mrammadaisavauhāraina laddhanāmaṃ  arañ‍ñaṃ| taṃ pana muṃrvānagarassa aisan‍nai pañ‍cadhanusatikai padaisai jātaṃ| ‘‘vissutai’’ti visaisaina kittitai| sārañ‍ca asārañ‍ca vivaicainti vicāraitvā jānantisīlaināti sārāsāra vivaikinauti vacanatthauti|

iti paramatthadīpaniyā anudīpanā niṭṭhitā|

1. paramatthadīpanī nāma, yaina thairaina sā katā|

tainaivai sā katā hauti, ayaṃ tassānu dīpanī|

2. aṭṭhasattadvayaikamhi, sākai sā jaiṭṭhamāsakai|

kāḷai navamiyaṃ divā, majjhanhikai niṭṭhaṃ gatā|

anudīpanī niṭṭhitā|
